# Quests
Aliases: Beginning of The End, Beginning of the End, Explore the Hub, Explore the hub, Flint Bros, Kuudra Quest, Kuudra quest, Quest, Quest Log, Shady Jake, Staying Informed, The Flint Bros, There Are Dwarves?, There are Dwarves?, Time to Strike, Warrior's Quest
Categories: Confirmations needed, More info needed
The Quest Log Interface
Quests are a gameplay mechanic that allows new players to explore the world and earn rewards for doing tasks. Different quests have different tasks that can require players to collect or find certain items or explore an area.
## Quest Log
The Quest Log provides information regarding the status of all of a player's quests. It can be opened through the [[SkyBlock Menu]] under the [[Quests & Chapters]] sub-menu and clicking on the Quest Log button represented by a [[Glitched Items|Book and Quill]]. Alternatively, the Quest Log menu is also accessible directly via the /quests command.

|Carpentry|The player must talk to the [[Carpenter]] in the [[Foraging Camp]], and then give him 64 (1 stack) White Wool ([[Wool]]). |Hub Explorer|The player must travel to 13 specific locations in the [[Hub Island]], including the [[Auction House]], [[Bank]], [[Library]], [[Village]], [[Colosseum]], [[Coal Mine]], [[Forest]], [[Farm]], [[Graveyard]], [[Wilderness]], [[Mountain]], [[Ruins]], and [[Unincorporated]].|Across the [[Hub Island]]||50 [[Coins]]; +5 SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|Visiting the [[Hub Island]]|
|Safety First|The player must reach SkyBlock Level 10 ([[SkyBlock Levels]]), and then talk to [[Susan]] to gain access to the [[Security Hall]]. |Chicken Racing ([[Chicken Race]])|The [[Chicken Race]] can be started by speaking to [[Gulliver]]. Reaching further along checkpoints will grant additional rewards, including a [[Red Gift]] for the first checkpoint in <33s, a [[Gift Compass]] for the first in <52s, the [[Frozen Chicken]] for the third in <68s, and [[Jingle Bells]] and +5 SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]]) for all four in <104s.|[[Jerry's Workshop]]|[[Gulliver]]|[[Red Gift]]; [[Gift Compass]]; [[Frozen Chicken]]; [[Jingle Bells]]; +5 SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]]) [Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)]|Visiting [[Jerry's Workshop]]|
|Dungeon Hub Racing ([[Dungeon Hub Race]])|The [[Dungeon Hub Race]] can be started by speaking to [[Guildford]] in the [[Dungeon Hub]]. There are three race types, including Crystal Core, Giant Mushrooms, Precursor Ruins. Each have four variation, including Anything, No Pearls, No Abilities, and Nothing at all, and four times that must be beaten, adding up to 96 total races. Completing the four times for one race grants the [[Cat Talisman]], for two races the [[Lynx Talisman]], and for three races the [[Cheetah Talisman]]. Each race completion also grants [[Wither Essence]], for a total of 432 (6 stacks plus 48) [[Wither Essence]].|[[Dungeon Hub]]|[[Guildford]]|[[Cat Talisman]]; [[Lynx Talisman]]; [[Cheetah Talisman]]; Up to 432 [[Wither Essence]]|Visiting the [[Dungeon Hub]]|

|Gearing Up|After completing Crop 'n Profit, the player must reach Garden IV (4) ([[The Garden]]), and then speak to [[Jeff]]. |Rooting for You|After unlocking Garden VII (7) ([[The Garden]]), the [[Carpenter]] will be able to visit [[The Garden]] as a Visitor ([[Garden Visitors]]). He will give the [[Greenhouse Blueprint]] in exchange for completing his offer, which can be given to [[Sam]] to unlock the [[Greenhouse]]. Converting the first plot to a Greenhouse will require 64 Enchanted Oak Logs ([[Enchanted Oak Log]]) and 128 (2 stacks) [[Enchanted Sand]]. Once placed, [[Sam]] will appear there, and give out a [[Carrot]] and [[HydroCan™ 1000]]. Placing the carrot and watering it will cause it quickly grow. Afterwards, Sam will give another 2 Carrots ([[Carrot]]), which can be placed next to each other to create a [[Dustgrain]] Mutation ([[Mutations]]). She also gives the [[Plant Diagnostics Tool]] to analyze this growth. Afterwards, she will explain how [[Mutations]] work, and give out 2 [[Seeds]] to create another [[Dustgrain]][Confirm]. Once harvested, speaking to her again will complete the quest, granting access to Greenhouse Upgrades ([[The Garden]]), 5,000 [[Coins]], +5,000 Farming XP ([[Farming]]), and +5 SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]]).|[[The Garden]]|[[Carpenter]] [[Sam]]|[[Greenhouse]] access; Greenhouse Upgrades ([[The Garden]]) access; 5,000 [[Coins]]; +5,000 Farming XP ([[Farming]]); +5 SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|[More Info Needed]|
|Shady Jake|[[Beth]] will visit [[The Garden]] as a visitor ([[Garden Visitors]]) multiple times. The first three times the player accepts their offer, she talks about investigating [[Jake]]. After the third visit, the player must dig at the coordinates 175, 42, -470 ([[Coordinates]]) in the [[Glowing Mushroom Cave]]. After the next visit, the player can meet [[Beth]] there, where [[Jake]] will show up. After a back and fourth conversation, the player will obtain [[Jake's Plushie]] and +5 SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]]).|[[The Garden]]; [[Overgrown Mushroom Cave]]; [[Underground Lab]]|[[Beth]] [[Jake]]|[[Jake's Plushie]]; +5 SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|[[Beth]] visits[Confirm]|
|Further Investigate Jake|After completing Shady Jake and unlocking the [[Greenhouse]], [[Beth]] can visit again on [[The Garden]]. After accepting her offer, the player will have to meet her at the [[Underground Lab]] again, and then pull a lever on the wall. This will bring the two to another room, where [[Jake]] will enter. He will talk about the [[Crop Analyzer]], and how it can be used to analyze [[Mutations]]. After this dialogue complestes, the quest will complete, granting access to the [[Crop Analyzer]] and +5 SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]]).|[[The Garden]]; [[Underground Lab]]|[[Beth]] [[Jake]]|[[Crop Analyzer]] access; +5 SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|Completing [[Beth]]'s fifth offer[Confirm]|
|Introduction to the Crop Analyzer|After completing Further Investigate Jake, the player must try and use the [[Crop Analyzer]]. [[Jake]] will intervene, saying that he hasn't explained the machine yet, and task the player to bringing him a [[Dustgrain]]. Upon doing so, he will task the player with analyzing the Dustgrain.|[[Underground Lab]]|[[Jake]]|[More Info Needed]|Completing Further Investigate Jake|
### Fishing
|Quest Name|Description|Locations|NPCs|Rewards|Menu Appearance Condition|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|Fishing Tutorial|The player must speak to [[Fisherwoman Enid]] in the [[Village]]. She will hand out a Fishing Rod ([[Fishing Rods]]), which can be used to catch a fish. |Finding the Engine|After completing Fishing Tutorial, the player must reach Fishing V (5) ([[Fishing]]), and then speak to [[Fisherman Gerald]]. |Hotspotting|The player must first speak to [[Hattie]] on the [[Backwater Bayou]], who will explain [[Fishing Hotspots]]. A hotspot will appear behind her, which the player must fish from. An additional +100 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]]) is obtained as a [[Chapters]] task reward.|[[Backwater Bayou]]|[[Hattie]]|1,000 [[Coins]]; +100 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]]); +5 SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]]); +100 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]]) ([[Chapters]])|Visiting the [[Backwater Bayou]]|

|Woods Racing ([[Woods Race]])|The player must first unlock the [[Jungle Island]], and then speak to [[Gustave]] in the [[Spruce Woods]]. He grants access to a race that starts next to him and ends atop the [[Jungle Island]]. Completing this race in 2 minutes will grant a [[Polished Pebble]], in 1 minute a [[Hunter Knife]], in 32 seconds a [[Wolf Paw]], and in 18 seconds a [[Silky Lichen]] and +5 SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]]).|[[Spruce Woods]]; [[Jungle Island]]|[[Gustave]]|[[Polished Pebble]]; [[Hunter Knife]]; [[Wolf Paw]]; [[Silky Lichen]]; +5 SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|Completing The Rebuild[Confirm]|
|Mole Problems|The player must speak to [[Molbert]] on the [[Jungle Island]], and then bring him 512 Jungle Logs ([[Jungle Log]]). After bringing doing, and then leaving and returning to the area, he will task the player with placing down three traps, then speaking to him and returning again. Afterwards, he will get stuck in one of the traps, which the player must free him from. Doing so will complete the quest, granting the [[Mole Hat]], 1,500 [[Coins]], +500 Foraging XP ([[Foraging]]), +5 SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]]), and access to the [[Moonglade Marsh]] upon reaching Foraging XII (12) ([[Foraging]]).|[[Jungle Island]]|[[Molbert]]|[[Mole Hat]]; 1,500 [[Coins]]; +500 Foraging XP ([[Foraging]]); +5 SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]]); [[Moonglade Marsh]] access (with Foraging XII (12) ([[Foraging]]))|Completing The Rebuild|
|Heart of the Forest|The player must speak to [[Hina]] in [[Tangleburg's Path]] on the [[Moonglade Marsh]] to unlock [[Heart of the Forest]] and complete the quest.|[[Tangleburg's Path]]|[[Hina]]|[[Heart of the Forest]] access|Visiting the [[Moonglade Marsh]]|
|Swoop's Instructions|After unlocking [[Heart of the Forest]], the player must speak to [[Swoop]] in [[Tangleburg]]. He will have the player destroy the berry beneath him, as well as cut a Fig Tree ([[Fig Log]]). Afterwards, he will explain the mechanics of [[Sweep]] and [[Tree Gifts]], completing the quest and granting 10,000 [[Coins]], +5,000 Foraging XP ([[Foraging]]), and +5 SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]]).|[[Tangleburg]]|[[Swoop]]|10,000 [[Coins]]; +5,000 Foraging XP ([[Foraging]]); +5 SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|Completing Heart of the Forest|
|David's Hunting Lessons|After completing Swoop's Instructions, the player must speak to [[David Hunterborough]]. He will teach the player about various [[Hunting]] mechanics, and have them obtain and try out [[Hunting]] items by speaking to [[Auryon]], [[Kysha]], and [[Agatha]].|[[Tangleburg]]; [[Evergreen Plateau]]; [[Fusion House]]; [[Murkwater Loch]]|[[David Hunterborough]] [[Auryon]] [[Kysha]] [[Agatha]]|[More Info Needed]|Completing Swoop's Instructions|
### Combat
|Quest Name|Description|Locations|NPCs|Rewards|Menu Appearance Condition|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|Time to Strike|The player must speak to the [[Bartender]] in the [[Combat Settlement]]. He will give the player an [[Undead Sword]], and then require 10 Graveyard Zombies ([[Graveyard Zombie]]) or Zombie Villagers ([[Zombie Villager]]) to be killed. Doing so will grant +5 SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]]) and +100 Combat XP ([[Combat]]). After speaking to the Bartender again, he grants 100 [[Coins]] and tasks the player with heading to the [[Spider's Den]]. Upon arriving, the player earns +10 Combat XP ([[Combat]]), and is then tasked with speaking to [[Haymitch]]. He tasks the player with entering the [[Spider Mound]] and collecting one [[String]] and one [[Spider Eye]] from the Mobs ([[Mob]]) there. Doing so grants an additional +15 Combat XP ([[Combat]]) and completes the quest.|[[Combat Settlement]]; [[Graveyard]]; [[Spider's Den]]; [[Spider Mound]]|[[Bartender]]; [[Haymitch]]|[[Undead Sword]]; 100 [[Coins]]; +125 Combat XP ([[Combat]]); +5 SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|Visiting the [[Hub Island]]|
|The Flint Bros|The player must talk to [[Rick]] in the [[Spider's Den]] and give him 2 Iron Ingots ([[Iron Ingot]]).|[[Spider's Den]]|[[Rick]]|[[Promising Shovel]] item and recipe|Visiting the [[Spider's Den]]|
|Shaggy's Quest|The player must first speak to [[Shaggy]] in [[Grandma's House]] on the [[Spider's Den]]. He will task the player with summoning [[Arachne]] using 4 Arachne's Callings ([[Arachne's Calling]]) or an [[Arachne Crystal]]. After doing that and speaking to him again, the quest will complete, and he will unlock a menu to purchase Arachne Crystals ([[Arachne Crystal]]).|[[Grandma's House]]; [[Arachne's Sanctuary]]|[[Shaggy]]|[[Arachne Crystal]] shop access|Visiting the [[Spider's Den]]|
|Arachne's Web|The player must speak to the [[Spider Tamer]] in [[Arachne's Sanctuary]]. Afterwards, they must help kill [[Arachne]] and speak to the tamer again. Doing so will complete the quest, granting 10 [[Spider Essence]] and access to the Spider Essence Shop ([[Essence Shops]]).|[[Arachne's Sanctuary]]|[[Spider Tamer]]|10 [[Spider Essence]]; Spider Essence Shop ([[Essence Shops]]) access|Visiting the [[Spider's Den]]|
|Relic ([[Relics]]) Hunting|The player must first speak to the [[Archaeologist]] in the [[Archaeologist's Camp]]. He will give the player an [[Archaeologist's Compass]], which will point to undiscovered [[Relics]]. Each found relic will grant 10,000 [[Coins]], and after finding all 28 of them, the player can speak to the Archaeologist again to complete the quest, granting another 30,000 [[Coins]].|[[Archaeologist's Camp]]; [[Spider's Den]]|[[Archaeologist]]|310,000 [[Coins]]|Visiting the [[Spider's Den]]|
|Beginning of The End|The player must speak to the [[Pearl Dealer]], and then mine 1 [[End Stone]]. Afterwards, traveling to the [[Dragon's Nest]] and being there when an [[Ender Dragon]] spawns will complete the quest, granting +100 Combat XP ([[Combat]]).|[[The End]]; [[Dragon's Nest]]|[[Pearl Dealer]]|+100 Combat XP ([[Combat]])|Visiting [[The End]]|
|The End Race ([[End Race]])|The player must speak to [[Guber]] in [[The End]], then stand on the pressure plate next to him to start the race. The race finishes after reaching the pressure plate on the other side of the island and returning. Completing the race in under 3 minutes will grant 8 Silent Pearls ([[Silent Pearl]]), in under 2 minutes an [[Enderman Mask]], in under 80 seconds a [[Nope the Fish]], and in under 48 seconds a [[Pig's Foot]] and +5 SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]]).|[[The End]]|[[Guber]]|8 Silent Pearls ([[Silent Pearl]]); [[Enderman Mask]]; [[Nope the Fish]]; [[Pig's Foot]]; +5 SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|Visiting [[The End]]|
|Talk to Lone Adventurer|The player first must talk to the [[Lone Adventurer]] in [[The End]], where they will instruct the player to kill 5 Endermen ([[Enderman]]). Once done, he will offer the player a [[Void Sword]] for 200,000 [[Coins]], which can also be purchased from [[Rusty]] if denied. Afterwards, he will move to the [[Dragon's Nest]] gate, and task the player with wearing all 8 pieces of [[Ender Armor]]. Once worn, he will task the player with killing Seers ([[Seer]]) until one drops a [[Dragon Shortbow]], and then bringing it to him. The player must then speak to the [[Pearl Dealer]] to claim the same bow for themselves. Once the player reaches Combat XVIII (18) ([[Combat]]), speaking to him again will complete the quest, and unlock a menu to view [[Ender Dragon]] stats.|[[The End]]|[[Lone Adventurer]] [[Pearl Dealer]]|[[Void Sword]] purchase; [[Dragon Shortbow]]|Visiting [[The End]]|
|Warrior's Quest|The player must reach Combat XXII (22) ([[Combat]]), and then travel to the [[Crimson Isle]]. [More Info Needed]|||[[Crimson Isle]] access|Visiting the [[Spider's Den]]|
|Select a Faction|The player must speak to [[Elle]], and then help kill the 3 Blazes ([[Blaze]]) attacking her. Afterwards, she will move towards the center of the [[Stronghold]], then task the player with bringing her 4 Magma Chunks ([[Magma Chunk]]), 3 Blaze Ashess ([[Blaze Ashes]]) and 2 [[Spectre Dust]]. Once done, Elle will leave the the [[Stronghold]] and appear in front of the [[Chief's Hut]] within [[Dragontail]], and outside the Community Center ([[Community Center (Scarleton)]]) within [[Scarleton]]. She will then task the player with speaking to four NPCs ([[NPC]]) from each Faction ([[Factions]]), so that they can make their mind up about which one they want to join. The quest then splits depending on which faction was chosen.|[[Crimson Isle]]; [[Stronghold]]; [[Dragontail]]; [[Scarleton]]|[[Elle]]; Other NPCs ([[NPC]])|Access to [[Factions]]|Visiting the [[Crimson Isle]]|
|Join the Mage Faction|As part of the [[Crimson Isle Main Questline]], the player must select to join the Mage Faction ([[Factions]]) by speaking to [[Elle]]. She will give the player a temporary [[Maddox Batphone]] to give to the [[Dean]], who will give a [[Letter of Recommendation]] in return. This can be given to [[Queen Nyx]] to join the faction.|[[Scarleton]]; [[Cathedral]]; [[Mage Council]]|[[Elle]] [[Dean]] [[Queen Nyx]]|Access to the Mage Faction ([[Factions]])|Completing Select a Faction|
|Purchase an Abiphone|As part of the [[Crimson Isle Main Questline]], the player must speak to [[Udel]], purchase an Abiphone ([[Abiphones]]), and then call the [[Dean]] on the Abiphone.|[[Scarleton Bank]]|[[Udel]] [[Dean]]|[[Dean]]'s Abiphone ([[Abiphones]]) contact|Completing Join the Mage Faction|
|Recover Lost Research|As part of the [[Crimson Isle Main Questline]], the player must speak to [[Elle]] in the [[Stronghold]], who will add her contact to the player's Abiphone ([[Abiphones]]). She will then task the player to infiltrate [[The Bastion]] and recover the stolen research, and then call the [[Dean]] on an Abiphone ([[Abiphones]]). He will thank the player and have them come to the [[Mage Council]] to discuss it, where the player will earn +100 ቾ Mage Reputation ([[Factions]]).|[[Stronghold]]; [[The Bastion]]; [[Mage Council]]|[[Elle]] [[Dean]] [[Queen Nyx]] [[Captain Ahone]] [[Counsellor]]|[[Elle]]'s Abiphone ([[Abiphones]]) contact; +100 ቾ Mage Reputation ([[Factions]])|Completing Purchase an Abiphone|
|Find the Dean's Apprentice|As part of the [[Crimson Isle Main Questline]], the player must speak to the [[Dean]] in the [[Cathedral]], where he will task the player with finding the Apprentice ([[Apprentice (Crimson Isle)]]). This will bring the player to the meet [[Captain Ahone]] in the [[Burning Desert]], who will give out their Abiphone ([[Abiphones]]) contact. Upon finding the Apprentice, the player must call them, which will cause them show up and splash healing potions on them. The player must then speak to the [[Dean]] again.|[[Cathedral]]; [[Burning Desert]]|[[Dean]] [[Captain Ahone]]|[[Captain Ahone]]'s Abiphone ([[Abiphones]]) contact|Completing Recover Lost Research|
|Help Dean|As part of the [[Crimson Isle Main Questline]], the player must bring the [[Dean]] 10 Magma Chunks ([[Magma Chunk]]) and 5 Burning Eyes ([[Burning Eye]]). This will grant +150 ቾ Mage Reputation ([[Factions]]). Afterwards, the player must help [[Igrupan]] found his 5 lost chickens, which will grant +50 ቾ Mage Reputation ([[Factions]]). The player must then speak to [[Odexar]], who will give a Volcanic Stonefish ([[Trophy Fish]]) to give to [[Odger]]. However, he will reject this stonefish, due to it being damaged, and task the player with fishing up a new one. Upon bringing this to [[Odger]], he will exchange it for a [[Framed Volcanic Stonefish]], which can be given to [[Odexar]] to obtain +100 ቾ Mage Reputation ([[Factions]]) and 80 [[Coins]]. The player must then speak to the [[Dean]] and bring him a [[Hallowed Skull]], [[Lumino Fiber]], and [[Leather Cloth]].|[[Cathedral]]; [[Igrupan's Chicken Coop]]; [[Scarleton]]; [[Odger's Hut]]|[[Dean]] [[Igrupan]] [[Odexar]] [[Odger]]|+300 ቾ Mage Reputation ([[Factions]]); 80 [[Coins]]|Completing Find the Dean's Apprentice|
|Recover Sulphur|As part of the [[Crimson Isle Main Questline]], the player must speak to [[Captain Ahone]], who will mention stolen [[Sulphur]]. This will start a meeting at the [[Forgotten Skull]], resulting in the player having to defeat [[Kuudra]]. Successfully defeating it will grant +200 ቾ Mage Reputation ([[Factions]]), and speaking to [[Dean]] afterwards will complete the quest and overall Mage Faction ([[Factions]]) storyline.|[[Forgotten Skull]]; [[Kuudra's Hollow]]|[[Captain Ahone]] [[Elle]] [[Ugo]] [[Crag]]|+200 ቾ Mage Reputation ([[Factions]]); [[Kuudra]] fight access|Completing Help Dean|
|Join the Barbarian Faction|As part of the [[Crimson Isle Main Questline]], the player must select to join the Barbarian Faction ([[Factions]]) by speaking to [[Elle]]. She will task the player with defeating the X Corrupted X ([[Corrupted Mobs]]) [[Flaming Spider]] in the [[Burning Desert]], and then using the obtained [[Sulphur]] on [[Ugo]]. The player will then have to kill nearby Mobs ([[Mob]]) to escort these two to the [[Chief's Hut]]. Upon then talking to [[Chief Scorn]] here, he will invite the player to the Barbarian Faction ([[Factions]]).|[[Crimson Isle]]; [[Burning Desert]]|[[Elle]] [[Ugo]] [[Chief Scorn]]|Access to the Barbarian Faction ([[Factions]])|Completing Select a Faction|
|Recover the Dean's Research|As part of the [[Crimson Isle Main Questline]], the player must speak to [[Ugo]] and [[Elle]], who will discuss having to steal research from the Mage Faction ([[Factions]]). Elle will then bring the player to the [[Cathedral]], where the player will have to steal the research from [[Dean]]'s office. Upon completion, speaking to [[Elle]], [[Ugo]], and then [[Chief Scorn]] will grant +100 ⚒️ Barbarian Reputation ([[Factions]]).|[[Dragontail]]; [[Cathedral]]|[[Ugo]] [[Elle]] [[Chief Scorn]]|+100 ⚒️ Barbarian Reputation ([[Factions]])|Completing Join the Barbarian Faction|
|Barbarian Training|As part of the [[Crimson Isle Main Questline]], the player will have to first complete [[Dojo]] tasks for [[Ugo]], and then defeat the [[Magma Boss]] for him. This will grant +150 ⚒️ Barbarian Reputation ([[Factions]]). Afterwards, the player will have to help [[Lasea]] by giving her a Skeleton Fish ([[Trophy Fish]]) and Flyfish ([[Trophy Fish]]) and speaking to [[Odger]], which will grant +100 ⚒️ Barbarian Reputation ([[Factions]]). This player must then speak to [[Gris]].|[[Dojo]]; [[Magma Chamber]]; [[Dragontail]]; [[Burning Desert]]|[[Ugo]] [[Lasea]] [[Odger]] [[Gris]]|+250 ⚒️ Barbarian Reputation ([[Factions]])|Completing Recover the Dean's Research|
|Recover Sulphur|As part of the [[Crimson Isle Main Questline]], the player must obtain a [[Heavy Pearl]] from [[The Matriarch]].|[[Matriarch's Lair]]||[[Heavy Pearl]]|Completing Barbarian Training|
|Recover Sulphur|As part of the [[Crimson Isle Main Questline]], the player must bring the obtained [[Heavy Pearl]] to [[Gris]], which will grant +150 ⚒️ Barbarian Reputation ([[Factions]]) and 200,000 [[Coins]].|[[Dragontail]]|[[Gris]]|+150 ⚒️ Barbarian Reputation ([[Factions]]); 200,000 [[Coins]]|Completing Recover Sulphur|
|Recover Sulphur|As part of the [[Crimson Isle Main Questline]], the player must speak to [[Chief Scorn]], who will mention stolen [[Sulphur]]. This will start a meeting at the [[Forgotten Skull]], resulting in the player having to defeat [[Kuudra]]. Successfully defeating it will grant +200 ⚒️ Barbarian Reputation ([[Factions]]), and speaking to [[Chief Scorn]] afterwards will grant another +200 ⚒️ Barbarian Reputation ([[Factions]]), completing the quest and overall Barbarian Faction ([[Factions]]) storyline.|[[Chief's Hut]]; [[Forgotten Skull]]; [[Kuudra's Hollow]]|[[Chief Scorn]] [[Captain Ahone]] [[Elle]] [[Ugo]] [[Crag]]|+400 ⚒️ Barbarian Reputation ([[Factions]]); [[Kuudra]] fight access|Completing Recover Sulphur|
## Removed Quests
The following quests were at one point in time in the game, but later removed.
|Quest Name|Description|Locations|NPCs|Rewards|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|Introduce Yourself|This quest required the player to speak to all 12 [[Hub Villagers]]. Each one granted 10 [[Coins]] when spoken to, and an additional 50 [[Coins]] was granted for completing the quest. Villagers that the player hasn't talked to yet will have green particles above their heads.|[[Hub Island]]|[[Hub Villagers]]|170 [[Coins]]|
|Fisherman's Quest|This quest required the player to speak to the Fisherman ([[Fisherman Gerald]]), and then collect and bring them 5 [[Clay Ball]]. Doing so would grant a [[Prismarine Rod]].|[[Fisherman's Hut]]|Fisherman ([[Fisherman Gerald]])|[[Prismarine Rod]]|
|Talk to the Villager Inhabitants[Confirm]|This quest required the player to speak to [[Mason]], [[Beth]], and the [[Friendly Hiker]] in the [[Desert Settlement]] of the [[Mushroom Desert]].[Confirm]|[[Desert Settlement]]|[[Mason]] [[Beth]] [[Friendly Hiker]]|[More Info Needed]|
|Warrior's Quest (V1)|This quest required the player to speak to [[Elle of the Nether]], and then bring her 1 [[Nether Wart]] and 1 [[Blaze Rod]]. Afterwards, the player would have to bring 1 [[Glowstone Dust]], 1 [[Nether Quartz]], and 1 [[Magma Cream]]. This would complete the quest and grant +15 Combat XP ([[Combat]]).|[[Blazing Fortress]]|[[Elle of the Nether]]|+15 Combat XP ([[Combat]])|
## Other Quests
Some [[NPC|NPCs]] have one-time interactions involving items that are not considered Quests or are not part of the Quest Log. Some of these include:
- [[Slayer]] quests
- [[Chapters]] tasks
- [[Rift Dimension]] quests
- [[Faction Quests]] and other means of gaining [[Factions|Reputation]] (such as [[Chickzilla]], [[Kaus]], or [[Rollim]])
- [[Romero and Juliette]]
- [[Cult of the Fallen Star]]
- [[Bednom]]
- [[Royal Resident]]
- [[Lucius]]
- Talking to [[Kat]] (sidebar objective text)
- Talking to [[George]] (sidebar objective text)
- Dialogue related to obtaining [[Abiphones|Abiphone]] contacts
- The [[NPC|NPCs]] involved in obtaining [[Kuudra Follower Armor]]
- Other tasks required to obtain certain items sold by [[Rusty]]
## Game Messages
|Message Example|Condition|
|-|-|
|NEW OBJECTIVE Give Ryan Dark Oak Logs|New Objective Example|
|QUEST COMPLETE REWARDS Campfire Initiate Badge I +2,000 Coins +1,000 Foraging Experience +5 SkyBlock XP|Quest Complete Example|
## Trivia
- The Beginning of The End quest was the first and only way to obtain Dungeoneering XP until 0.8.

# Sea Creatures
Aliases: Any Water Source, Anywhere, Bayou Sea Creature, Lava Sea Creature, Lava Sea Creatures, Lava sea creature, Scg, Sea Creature, Sea Creature Guide, Sea creatures, Spooky Sea Creature, Spooky sea creature, Winter Sea Creatures
Categories: Confirmations needed, Pages with UIs, Fishing, Mobs
Sea Creatures are mobs which can be caught while fishing. The likelihood of catching Sea Creatures is increased by [[Sea Creature Chance]], which starts at 20 .
## Mechanics
Sea Creatures are mobs which can be caught while fishing. The type of Sea Creature which is caught depends on the player's [[Fishing]] level, the bobber location and fishing medium ( [[Vanilla Objects/Blocks|Water]] or [[Vanilla Objects/Blocks|Lava]]). When killed, they drop loot and [[Fishing]] XP.
- The list of all Sea Creatures, their requirements to be caught and drops can be seen in the in-game Sea Creature Guide through the Fishing Skill menu or the /scg command.
- The chance in percentage to catch a Sea Creature is determined by the player's [[Sea Creature Chance]]. The chance of catching a Sea Creature is reduced on [[Private Island]] and [[The Garden]] though this change is not reflected in the [[Stats]] menu.
- Sea Creatures cannot be damaged by arrows, except those shot from the [[Prismarine Bow]]. They take 2x damage from [[Fishing Rods]].
- There is a Sea Creature cap of 60 per server (30 on [[Galatea]][Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)]). After this cap is reached, players will only be able to catch fish and treasure, even with 100 [[Sea Creature Chance]], until some Sea Creatures are killed or despawn. Furthermore, there is a per-player Sea Creature cap of 10 on all islands.
  - If a player already has 9 Sea Creatures out and catches two at once due to [[Double Hook Chance]], this cap can be exceeded, making the theoretical maximum per player 11 Sea Creatures. However, [[Magma Slug|Magma Slugs]] and [[Water Hydra|Water Hydras]] are an exception. Despite being split up to multiple entities, it still only counts as one towards the cap.
  - Upon reaching the cap, players will receive the following message: There is not enough space for another Sea Creature! Kill some to make space for new ones!
- Sea Creatures despawn if not killed within 6 minutes, or if the player who fished them up leaves the server. The [[Magma Slug|Baby Magma Slug]] is an exception to this as it does not despawn naturally after being split up to three.
## Spawn Mechanics
### Weight
Sea Creature Weight is a concept where special numbers are distributed to each sea creature for catch possibility calculations. Sea creatures with higher weight numbers are more likely to be caught up than that of lower weight numbers.
The catch rate for each sea creature can be calculated with this formula: CatchRate=SeaCreatureWeightCombinedWeightOfAllAvailableSeaCreatures×100% Several items can also affect the weight of sea creatures:
|Modifier|Weight|Affected Sea Creatures|
|-|-|-|
|[[Whale Bait]]|1.25x|Base Weight is lower than 400|
|[[Light Bait]]|1.25x|Base Weight is lower than 400 during daytime|
|[[Dark Bait]]|1.25x|Base Weight is lower than 400 during nighttime|
|[[Common Hook]]|1.25x|COMMON ([[Rarity]]) Sea Creatures|
|[[Puddle Jumper Hook]]|5x|[[Puddle Jumper]]|
|[[Squid Hat]]|2x|[[Squid]], [[Inkling]]|
|Drake Piper I Perk ([[Essence Shops]])|1.1x|[[Reindrake]]|
|[[Tracking]]|Varies|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]]) and MYTHIC ([[Rarity]]) Sea Creatures|
### Pools
Basic Water Sea Creatures can be caught in most [[Vanilla Objects/Blocks|Water]].
- They are replaced with island pools on [[Galatea]], [[Jerry's Workshop]], [[Backwater Bayou]] and [[Lotus Atoll]].
- Event, Hotspot and certain Special Sea Creatures have a chance to replace other sea creatures in other pools:
Spooky Sea Creatures have a 15% chance to override other Sea Creature catches in [[Vanilla Objects/Blocks|Water]] while [[Fear Mongerer]] is in the [[Village]], which can be increased by modifiers:
|Modifier|Chance (up to 75%)|
|-|-|
|Spooky Savant V Perk ([[Doug]])|+5%|
|[[Spooky Bait]]|+15%|
|[[Spooky Hook]]|+20%|
|MYTHIC ([[Rarity]]) [[Bat Pet]]|+20%|
Fishing Festival Sea Creatures have a 15% chance to override other Sea Creature catches in [[Vanilla Objects/Blocks|Water]] during [[Fishing Festival]], which can be increased by modifiers:
|Modifier|Chance (up to 80%)|
|-|-|
|Shark Sonar V Perk ([[Doug]])|+5%|
|[[Shark Bait]]|+20%|
|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]]) [[Megalodon Pet]]|+20%|
|[[Shark Scale Armor]]|+20%|
Hotspot Sea Creatures have a 15% chance to override other Sea Creature catches in [[Vanilla Objects/Blocks|Water]] or [[Vanilla Objects/Blocks|Lava]] when fishing in [[Hotspot (disambiguation)|Hotspots]], which can be increased by modifiers:
|Modifier|Chance (up to 85%)|
|-|-|
|[[Hotspot Tonic Mixin]]|+10%|
|[[Hotspot Hook]]|+20%|
|[[Hotspot Bait]]|+20%|
|MYTHIC ([[Rarity]]) [[Hermit Crab Pet]]|+20%|
|Sea Creature|Base Chance|Requirement|Liquid|Modifiers|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Agarimoo]]|15%|[[Empty Chumcap Bucket]]|Water ([[Vanilla Objects/Blocks]])||
|[[Carrot King]]|5%|[[Carrot Bait]]|Water ([[Vanilla Objects/Blocks]])||
|[[Plhlegblast]]|0.1%|[[Plhlegblast Pool]]|Lava ([[Vanilla Objects/Blocks]])|[[Squid Hat]] (+0.1%)|

## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jul 2, 2019|0.5|Added Sea Creatures.|
|Jun 26, 2023|Fixed some Sea Creatures not giving Fishing EXP when killed.|
|Jun 29, 2023|Fixed not getting Fishing EXP from killing Sea Creatures under very specific circumstances.|
|Aug 1, 2023|0.19.2|Fixed Sea Creature counter not incrementing if someone else killed your Sea Creature.|
|Aug 1, 2023|0.19.2|Players deal 1% to legendary sea creatures caught by others for the first five seconds.|
|Aug 15, 2023|Sea Creatures now despawn when their "owner" disconnects or leaves the server. This fixes a bug where you could change profiles and earn Fishing EXP on a different profile.|
|Jul 2, 2024|0.20.3|Added a personal Sea Creature cap of 20 in the [[Crystal Hollows]] and 5 in the [[Crimson Isle]].|
|May 6, 2026|0.24.5|Added a personal Sea Creature cap of 10 to every island.|
|May 6, 2026|0.24.5|Added a chat message when reaching Sea Creature cap.|
|May 26, 2026|0.25|Enabled the ability to catch [[Galatea]] Sea Creatures and more [[Backwater Bayou]] Sea Creatures on ☀ Stranded ([[Stranded]]).|
|May 26, 2026|0.25|Disabled the ability to catch [[Backwater Bayou]] and [[Galatea]] Sea Creatures on ☀ Stranded ([[Stranded]]).|

# Slayer
Aliases: Auto Slayer, Auto slayer, Autoslayer, Slayer Boss, Slayer Bosses, Slayer Quest, Slayer Quests, Slayer Veteran Bonus, Slayer Xp, Slayer xp, Slayers
Categories: Confirmations needed, Outdated pages, More info needed, Slayer, Mechanics
[Slayer]
Slayers are [[Combat]]-oriented missions that involve killing a specific type of mob and gaining [[Combat|Combat XP]], culminating with defeating a [[Slayer|SLAYER BOSS]].
## Overview
Slayer Quests can be started for a [[Coins]] fee by talking to [[Maddox the Slayer]] under the [[Tavern]] at [[Coordinates|-83.5, 68, -129.5]], or remotely using a [[Maddox Batphone]] or [[Abiphones|Abiphone]].
Only 1 Slayer Quest can be active at a time.
Once a Slayer Quest is started, the specified amount of [[Combat|Combat XP]] must be earned from killing the Slayer's respective mob:
- This amount of [[Combat|Combat XP]] increases with the tier of the Slayer Quest, but so does the [[Coins]] fee.
- Tier III+ Slayer Quests may also spawn [[Minibosses|SLAYER MINIBOSS]], which are stronger and grant significantly more [[Combat|Combat XP]].
- Tier V [[Slayer|SLAYER BOSSES]] often have different names and express unique traits.
Defeating a [[Slayer|SLAYER BOSS]] will complete the Slayer Quest: the player can then claim Slayer XP, which increases the player's Slayer level for its corresponding Slayer.
- Slayer Levels unlock crafting recipes and permanent stat boosts, with some granting unique perks.
Defeating a [[Slayer|SLAYER BOSS]] will also award the following:
- Token drops: they are [[Drop Chance|1Guaranteed]], drop in varying numbers and are used in lots of recipes unlocked from its corresponding slayer.
  - Token drops have a compacted form that are used in more recipes, crafted using another commodity: for example, [[Tarantula Silk]] is crafted using [[Tarantula Web|Tarantula Webs]] and [[Enchanted Flint]].
  - Most Tier V variants of each [[Slayer|SLAYER BOSS]] have a chance to drop the upgraded form of their respective Token drop. For example, the [[Revenant Horror|Atoned Horror]] has a chance to drop [[Revenant Viscera|Revenant Visceras]].
- And possibly, rare and powerful items…
|Slayers|SLAYER BOSSES ([[Slayer]])|Token drop|Upgraded form of Token drop|
|-|-|-|-|
|Zombie ([[Zombie Slayer]])|[[Revenant Horror]] and Atoned Horror ([[Revenant Horror]])|[[Revenant Flesh]]|[[Revenant Viscera]]|
|Spider ([[Spider Slayer]])|[[Tarantula Broodfather]] and Primordial Broodfather ([[Tarantula Broodfather]])|[[Tarantula Web]]|[[Tarantula Silk]]|
|Wolf ([[Wolf Slayer]])|[[Sven Packmaster]]|[[Wolf Tooth]]|[[Golden Tooth]]|
|Enderman ([[Enderman Slayer]])|[[Voidgloom Seraph]]|[[Null Sphere]]|[[Null Ovoid]]|
|Vampire ([[Vampire Slayer]])|[[Riftstalker Bloodfiend]]|[[Coven Seal]]|[[Bloodbadge]]|
|Blaze ([[Blaze Slayer]])|[[Inferno Demonlord]]|[[Derelict Ashe]]|[[Molten Powder]]|
|Guardian ([[Guardian Slayer]])|Upcoming|Upcoming|Upcoming|
## Slayer Types and Requirements

## Leveling Slayer
Each Slayer track requires a certain amount of XP earned from killing the [[Slayer|SLAYER BOSS]] to progress. Killing a [[Slayer|SLAYER BOSS]] summoned by another player does not count towards the XP requirement. The amount of XP needed is as follows:
|Slayer|LVL 1|LVL 2|LVL 3|LVL 4|LVL 5|LVL 6|LVL 7|LVL 8|LVL 9|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|Zombie|5 XP|15 XP|200 XP|1,000 XP|5,000 XP|20k XP|100k XP|400k XP|1m XP|
|Spider|5 XP|25 XP|200 XP|1,000 XP|5,000 XP|20k XP|100k XP|400k XP|1m XP|
|Wolf|10 XP|30 XP|250 XP|1,500 XP|5,000 XP|20k XP|100k XP|400k XP|1m XP|
|Enderman|10 XP|30 XP|250 XP|1,500 XP|5,000 XP|20k XP|100k XP|400k XP|1m XP|
|Blaze|10 XP|30 XP|250 XP|1,500 XP|5,000 XP|20k XP|100k XP|400k XP|1m XP|
|Vampire|20 XP|75 XP|240 XP|840 XP|2,400 XP|||||
### Rewards
Players receive various rewards for reaching certain Slayer levels:
- Slayer Level - Earning levels in each slayer category will award a permanent stat boost.
- Global Combat Wisdom Buff - Earn extra [[Combat Wisdom]] based on unique [[Slayer|SLAYER BOSS]] kills. Beating a new [[Slayer|SLAYER BOSS]] on Tier I, Tier II, and Tier III will give +1 [[Combat Wisdom]], while Tier IV and Tier V bosses will give +2 [[Combat Wisdom]]. The current maximum bonus is a permanent +36 [[Combat Wisdom]].
- Slayer Bonus Rewards
  - Earn +3 of any [[Slayer|SLAYER BOSS]]'s main token drop when slaying its [[Minibosses|SLAYER MINIBOSSES]]. This bonus is unlocked upon reaching LVL 6 in Zombie, Wolf, and Spider Slayer.
  - Slayers are 4% cheaper. This bonus is unlocked upon reaching LVL 7 in Zombie, Spider, and Wolf Slayer.
- Level 8 Perks - Each slayer path unlocks a special perk unique to that path when the player reaches level 8.
  - [[Enderman Slayer]], notably, unlocks a perk at Level 9.
- Bartender Mixins - A Mixin from the [[Bartender]] is unlocked upon reaching LVL 8 in a slayer. These Mixins can be brewed with [[God Potion|God Potions]] to give them bonus special effects: Mixins can be directly consumed if a [[Booster Cookie|Cookie Buff]] is active.
- SkyBlock Levels - Upon gaining a Slayer Level or defeating all tiers of [[Slayer|SLAYER BOSS]] in one category, players unlock [[SkyBlock Levels|SkyBlock XP]].
- [[Bits]] - Depending on the tier, each [[Slayer|SLAYER BOSS]] will give a certain amount of [[Bits]].
[[Bits]] & [[Slayer|SLAYER BOSS]] Tier:
Tier: [[Bits]] earned
Tier I: 2 [[Bits]]
Tier II: 5 [[Bits]]
Tier III: 8 [[Bits]]
Tier IV: 12 [[Bits]]
Tier V: 15 [[Bits]]
### Healing
When the player defeats a Slayer boss, excluding [[Vampire Slayer]], they gain temporary [[Healing]] based on the [[Slayer|SLAYER BOSS]] tier and their Slayer level.
- The amount of [[Health]] gained per second is determined by the [[Slayer|SLAYER BOSS]] tier.
- The duration of the healing is determined by the player's Slayer level for that [[Slayer|SLAYER BOSS]] type.
Healing & [[Slayer|SLAYER BOSS]] Tier:
Tier: [[Health]] per Second
Tier I: 40 [[Health]]
Tier II: 80 [[Health]]
Tier III: 120 [[Health]]
Tier IV: 160 [[Health]]
Tier V: 200 [[Health]]
Healing & [[Slayer|SLAYER BOSS]] Level:
Level: Duration in Seconds
0-1: 6
2-3: 7
4-5: 8
6-7: 9
8-9: 10
## Auto-Slayer
Auto-Slayer is a feature unlocked upon reaching LVL 6 in [[Zombie Slayer]], [[Spider Slayer]], and [[Wolf Slayer]]. When enabled, players will automatically start a new Slayer Quest of the same type upon killing a [[Slayer|SLAYER BOSS]], automatically deducting coins from the player's bank.
Auto-Slayer will only start a new quest of the player has sufficient [[Coins]] in their purse or [[Bank]], with the purse taking priority over the Bank. If neither the player's purse or Bank have sufficient [[Coins]], Auto-Slayer will not begin a new quest.
Auto-Slayer can be toggled in the Slayer menu.

### Loot Pools
[[Slayer|SLAYER BOSSES]] drop loot by rolling from 4 different Loot Pools (or Loot Tables): Token, Main, Extra and Separate.
|Loot Pool|Description|Examples|
|-|-|-|
|Token|1Guaranteed ([[Drop Chance]]) drops that are used in various recipes and can be crafted into their respective upgraded form.|[[Revenant Flesh]]; [[Coven Seal]]|
|Main|Majority of a SLAYER BOSS ([[Slayer]])'s loot. They typically have drop rates below 20%.|[[Scythe Blade]]; [[Primordial Shard]]|
|Extra|Composed of [[Runes]].|Enchant Rune ([[Runes]]); Fiery Burst Rune ([[Runes]])|
|Separate|Only consists of the [[Paragon Shard]] since the 0.27 version - Torrhus Canyon release.|[[Paragon Shard]]|
### Rolling for Loot
- The order in which Loot Pools are rolled is as follows: Token → Main → Extra. The newly-added Separate one is also rolled.
- The order in which Modifiers are applied is as follows, if applicable: [[Pity and RNG Meters|RNG Meter]] → [[Magic Find]].
  - Modifiers effects are not carried between Loot Pools. Instead, Modifiers are re-applied for each Loot Pool.
- Restrictions from Slayer level and the [[Slayer|SLAYER BOSS]] tier apply:
  - For Slayer level: only items that are unlocked are accounted for their Item Weight. This means the Percent Drop Rate of an item can be inflated from a low Slayer level.
  - For [[Slayer|SLAYER BOSS]] tier: items that only drop from higher-tiered bosses will not drop from lower-tiered bosses, even if the [[Pity and RNG Meters|RNG Meter]] is full.
    - For example, a [[Judgement Core]] cannot drop from an Tier III [[Voidgloom Seraph]].
Formulas and Weight Modifiers for a certain Slayer drop:
|Its Loot Pool|Formula for its Percent Drop Rate|Modifiers [[Looting]]|Modifiers RNG Meter ([[Pity and RNG Meters]])|Modifiers [[Magic Find]]|Modifiers [[Hunting Fortune]]|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|Token|100|✕|✕|✕|✕|
|Main|100×Item Weight∑𝐓𝐨𝐤𝐞𝐧Weight+∑𝐌𝐚𝐢𝐧Weights|✕|✔|✔|✕|
|Extra|100×Item Weight∑𝐓𝐨𝐤𝐞𝐧Weight+∑𝐌𝐚𝐢𝐧Weights+∑𝐄𝐱𝐭𝐫𝐚Weights|✕|✔|✔|✕|
|Separate|Flat chances, see Slayer#Separate Loot Pool|✕[Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)]|✔|✔[Confirm].|✕|
#### Terminology
- Item Weight is the original weight, the "base" weight, before modifiers are applied.
- New Weight1 is the Item Weight after application of the [[Pity and RNG Meters|RNG Meter]].
- New Weight2 is the New Weight1 after application of [[Magic Find]]. It is also called the Percent Drop Chance or the Final Drop Chance.
#### Token Loot Pool
The first item to drop will be a Token, with a 100% drop rate.
#### Main Loot Pool
Then, the Main Loot Pool:
1. If the [[Pity and RNG Meters|RNG Meter]] is set on an item, then its Item Weight will be modified according to the following formula:
  - New Weight1=Item Weight×(1+min⁡(2×Stored Slayer XPRequired Slayer XP,2))
  Otherwise, New Weight1=Item Weight.
1. If an item's New Weight1<5%, then it will be modified according to the following formula:
  - New Weight2=Item Weight1×(1+Magic Find100)
  Otherwise, New Weight2=New Weight1.
1. Items in the Main Loot Pool will then roll, weighted with the Token Loot Pool.
  If a Main item is successfully rolled, it will drop. This also prevents any further successful Main roll. If the Token item is successfully rolled, nothing will drop.
#### Extra Loot Pool
Then, the Extra Loot Pool. Note that the modified weights from the Main Loot Pool are not carried over.
1. If the [[Pity and RNG Meters|RNG Meter]] is set on an item, then its Item Weight will be modified according to the following formula:
  - New Weight1=Item Weight×(1+min⁡(2×Stored Slayer XPRequired Slayer XP,2))
  Otherwise, New Weight1=Item Weight.
1. If an item's New Weight1<5%, then it will be modified according to the following formula:
  - New Weight2=Item Weight1×(1+Magic Find100)
  Otherwise, New Weight2=New Weight1.
1. Items in the Extra Loot Pool will then roll, weighted with the Token and Main Loot Pools.
  If a Main item is successfully rolled, it will drop. This also prevents any further successful Main roll[Confirm]. If a Token or a Main item is successfully rolled, nothing will drop.
#### Separate Loot Pool
- Its application order is unknown.
- As of version 0.27, its only item is the [[Paragon Shard]], which uses a flat chance.
- The [[Pity and RNG Meters|RNG Meters]] can target it but will not increase this chance.
  Separate Loot Pool Item: [[Paragon Shard]], Base Drop Rate: 0.25% (1 in 400), Conditions: Tier IV SLAYER BOSSES ([[Slayer]]) excepted [[Riftstalker Bloodfiend]]; Slayer V (5) and above for their respective Slayer.; Item: [[Paragon Shard]], Base Drop Rate: 0.5% (1 in 200), Conditions: Tier V SLAYER BOSSES ([[Slayer]]) excepted [[Riftstalker Bloodfiend]]; Slayer V (5) and above for their respective Slayer.
## RNG Meter
The RNG Meter is unlocked for each individual [[Slayer|SLAYER BOSS]] type upon slaying their respective Tier III [[Slayer|SLAYER BOSS]]. Slayer XP gained from killing Tier III, Tier IV, and Tier V [[Slayer|SLAYER BOSSES]] will increment their corresponding [[Pity and RNG Meters|RNG Meter]]. Players can select a drop in the RNG Meter, increasing the chances of that item dropping: upon filling the meter, the player is guaranteed to drop their selected item on the [[Slayer|SLAYER BOSS]] of an eligible tier.
## Trivia
- [[Minibosses|SLAYER MINIBOSSES]] despawn after not being interacted with after [More Info Needed] (More infomation needed; When this info is added, remove this.).
- In order to get a Slayer IX (9) from scratch and without external bonuses nor fails, it would take:
  - 2000 Tier IV, costing 100,000,000 [[Coins]];
  - or 200 Tier IV and 600 Tier V, costing 70,000,000 [[Coins]].
- Contributing 24 times to the [[City Projects|Bartender's Brewery]] [[City Projects|City Project]] granted a 5% discount on [[Slayer|Slayers]], overriding the 4% discount perk gained from reaching LVL 7 on the first 3 [[Slayer|Slayers]].
- Should a server reboots when a player has a Slayer [[Bosses|BOSS]] summoned, it will despawn and progress towards the Slayer Quest will be set to the maximum amount of [[Combat|Combat XP]]. The next mob killed will immediately spawn it again, providede that the mob was eligible to contribute to the Slayer Quest progress.
- When [[Enderman Slayer]] was released, all players lost access to Auto-Slayer due to not having [[Enderman Slayer|Enderman Slayer VI (6)]]. However, players who had it on before the update would be able to use it, and would not be able to disable it.
- Due to [[Magic Find]] only affecting the drops under a 5% chance, getting higher [[Magic Find]] will not push the drop chance to 100%. Instead:
  - For any item affected by [[Magic Find]]: limMF→+∞Percent Drop Chance=New Weight1∑MF-affectedNew Weights1
  - For any other non-[[Drop Chance|1Guaranteed]] item: limMF→+∞Percent Drop Chance=0.

# Soulbound
Aliases: Co-op Soulbound, Soulbound Item
Categories: Confirmations needed
Soulbound Items are items that cannot be transferred to other players, or other players outside of the player's [[Co-op]] (if they have one). This means that they cannot be sold to the Auction House or Bazaar. There are two types of Soulbound items.
## Types
- Player (Labeled as Soulbound): Cannot be placed in [[Chest|Chests]] on [[Private Island]], [[Auction House|Auctioned]], or [[Trading|Traded]].
- Co-op (Labeled as Co-op Soulbound): Cannot be [[Auction House|Auctioned]], or [[Trading|Traded]] to players outside of the player's [[Co-op]].
## Soulbound Items
There are several different ways for items to become Soulbound:
|Type|Description|Co-op Soulbound|
|-|-|-|
|[[Museum]]|Items that have donated to the [[Museum]] (besides Special Items) will automatically be Co-op Soulbound. The items can be taken out of the [[Museum]] at any time, and can be used normally, except for the new restrictions placed by the Soulbinding.|✔|
|Bingo Card ([[Bingo (disambiguation)]])|Bingo Cards ([[Bingo (disambiguation)]]) are Co-op Soulbound.|✔|
|Trophies ([[Trophy (disambiguation)]])|All types of trophies are Soulbound.|✕|
|Quest Items|Many items obtained from [[Quests]] and are part of [[Quests]] are usually Soulbound. If they are lost, they can be repurchased from [[Rusty]] for [[Coins]], and will be automatically Soulbound.|✕|
|[[Carnival]] Trophies|Participating in the carnival rewards 3 SPECIAL ([[Rarity]]) items that display the player's score and position in each minigame.|✔|
|[[Accessories]]|Certain accessories are Soulbound as they are one-time rewards, some of them can be repurchased from [[Rusty]] in case of losing said accessories.|✕|
|Legacy Items|Items that are no longer obtainable by normal gameplay may become Soulbound.|✔|
|[[Cake Soul]]|Captured Player Cake Souls become Soulbound.|✔|
## Trivia
- Certain items, like [[Talisman of Power]] and it's upgrades, are Soulbound despite not being stated so. [Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)]
- For a long time, most [[Crimson Isle]] drops were Co-op Soulbound, this has been changed since version 0.20.9.

# Spawnable Islands
Aliases: Spawnable Island, Spawnable islands, Spawning Islands, Spawning islands
Categories: Confirmations needed, Spawnable islands
Spawnable Islands are small pre-built islands you can spawn on your [[Private Island]].
Each comes with a [[Floating Crystals|floating crystal]] that provides a special benefit. Crystals will only appear if no others of the same type are on the island, and trying to pick one up and then placing it again after a new one is generated will be prevented.
When used, the edge of the closest island will spawn 15 blocks away in the direction you are facing. Notably, the Nether Wart Island spawns with it's entrance on the farthest side from you, backwards compared to what might be expected.
There is an achievement for placing 5 unique islands on your own private island.

See specific island pages for detailed breakdown of island contents.

|[[Nether Wart Island]]|[[Nether Wart Island]]|Island Contents: [[Soul Sand]], [[Obsidian]], [[Nether Bricks]], [[Nether Brick Stairs]], [[Nether Brick Slab]], [[Nether Wart]]|(same as above)|
|[[Roofed Forest Island]]|[[Roofed Forest Island]]|Bat Crystal ([[Floating Crystals]]) - Spawns Bats ([[Bat (disambiguation)]]), and regrows the Trees that come with the Island. Radius: 20|Dark Oak Log VI (6) ([[Dark Oak Log]]); Recipe: [[Dark Oak Log]] x5 ×3 + [[Dirt]] x5 ×6 → [[Roofed Forest Island]]|

# Spooky
Aliases: Spooky Mob, Spooky Mobs
Categories: Confirmations needed, Mob Types
[Spooky]
Type: Mob Type ([[Mob Types]])
Most commonly found during the Spooky Festival.
— Ike

There are many ways to increase stats against Spooky enemies.

|[[Bat Pet]]|[[Bat Pet]]|Pet ([[Pets]])|Wings of Steel: Deals +0.5-50% [[Damage]] to Spooky enemies during the [[Spooky Festival]].|Multiplicative (Multiplicative buffs are simple multipliers applied at the end, after flat and additive buffs.)|
|[[Ghoul Buster]]|[[Ghoul Buster]]|Weapon ([[Weapons]])|Ability: Spooktacular: Deal +100% more [[Damage]] to Spooky enemies during the [[Spooky Festival]].|Additive[Confirm]|

# Stranded
Aliases: Fletcher
Categories: Confirmations needed, Pages with UIs, Templates with deprecated argument names, More info needed
This page is missing information on several changes made to the game since March 2023, so parts of the page are outdated. You can help by contributing relevant information to this page and its subpages. This article was last edited by TDVoldy (contribs|logs|block) on 12:19, October 18 2026 (9 days ago)
Stranded is a [[Gamemodes|Gamemode]] where the player can only travel between their [[Private Island]] and [[The Garden]]. Additionally, Stranded profiles are similar to [[Ironman|♲ Ironman]] profiles in that they do not allow [[Trading]] with players outside of the [[Co-op]], they use the [[Bazaar|Special Bazaar]], and the [[Auction House]] cannot be accessed on Stranded.
The gamemode was officially declared End of Life on August 13, 2025 and will receive one final update to fix some of the most affecting issues.
## Overview
Stranded profiles can be played Solo or as a Co-op. Players on Stranded profiles are restricted to their [[Private Island]] and [[The Garden|Garden]] for the entirety of the game, so there are several changes to make parts of the game's content obtainable. Players are able to place down certain NPCs on their island, such as [[Jacob]] and [[Maddox the Slayer]], to make their respective content available. NPCs can be unlocked by reaching a specific milestone, such as reaching a certain [[Skills|Skill]] level or obtaining a specific item. Players can also trade with Villagers, crops grow faster on the [[Private Island]], and plenty of requirements such as [[Dungeons|Dungeon]] Requirements and [[Factions|Faction]] Requirements on items and minions are ignored.
## Mobs
On Stranded profiles, the level of some naturally spawning hostile mobs is the same as the player's [[Combat]] level. For example, a player with [[Combat|Combat VII (7)]] will have mostly level 7 monsters spawn on their island. All monsters require darkness to spawn. [[Runecrafting|Runic Mobs]] can also spawn on Stranded profiles. Below is the list of mobs that can spawn on Stranded profiles.

|[Lv157]|[[Ghost]]|Spawns in Plains Biome ([[Biome Stick]]) under Y level 40 on Light Gray ([[Stained Glass]]), Gray ([[Stained Glass]]) or Black Stained Glass ([[Stained Glass]]) at Combat XXX (30) ([[Combat]]) and above.[Confirm]||
|[Lv135]|[[Star Sentry]]|Spawns on Purple Wool ([[Wool]]) or [[Stained Glass]] within a Taiga Biome ([[Biome Stick]]).|Tip: Glass requires less dye than wool to craft, and only allows the desired mob to spawn on it.|
|[Lv61]|[[Treasure Hoarder]]|Spawns on Yellow Wool ([[Wool]]) or [[Stained Glass]] within a Taiga Biome ([[Biome Stick]]).|(same as above)|
|[Lv23]|[[Goblin]]|Spawns on Green Wool ([[Wool]]) or [[Stained Glass]] within a Taiga Biome ([[Biome Stick]]).|(same as above)|
|[Lv99]|[[Golden Goblin]]|Spawns from throwing Goblin Eggs ([[Goblin Egg]])||
|[Lv109]|[[Diamond Goblin]]|Spawns from throwing Goblin Eggs ([[Goblin Egg]])||

|[Lv28-312]|[[Spooky Festival]]|During the [[Spooky Festival]], Spooky Mobs ([[Spooky]]) spawn in dark areas at night. Spooky Sea Creatures ([[Fishing]]) can be fished up. Bat Piñatas ([[Bat Piñata]]) spawn occasionally on the island. [[Headless Horseman]] can be summoned using [[Horseman's Candle]].||

## Collections
Many collection materials cannot be obtained in the same way as in the Classic and Ironman gamemode. Once enough materials have been collected, Minions are usually a very useful way to obtain more of the same material. Below is a table with information on how to progress in all collections. To see the spawning requirements for a mob in the table, click on the corresponding link.
Show/Hide Collection List
e [[Stranded/Collections|v]]
|Collection|How to Obtain & Other Notes|
|-|-|
[Farming Collections]
|[[Cactus]]|Generated by the [[Desert Crystal]] on the [[Desert Island]]. Prior to reaching Cactus V (5) ([[Cactus]]), the Desert Island can be obtained from Villagers. 2 [[Enchanted Cactus Green]] is a guaranteed drop from [[Mite]] Pests on [[The Garden]] and can be used to paste a plot.|
|[[Carrot]]|1% chance to be dropped by Zombies. 1Guaranteed ([[Drop Chance]]) drop from Frosty ([[Frosty (mob)]]). After obtaining at least one, they can then be planted on farmland. 3 [[Enchanted Carrot]] is a guaranteed drop from [[Cricket]] Pests on [[The Garden]] and can be used to paste a plot.|
|[[Cocoa Beans]]|There are Cocoa Beans on jungle trees, which can be grown from Jungle Saplings. 3 [[Enchanted Cocoa Beans]] are a guaranteed drop from [[Moth]] Pests on [[The Garden]] and can be used to paste a plot.|
|[[Feather]]|Dropped by Chickens.|
|[[Leather]]|Dropped by Cows.|

|[[Mushroom]]|Obtained from the [[Roofed Forest Island]], which is crafted from a recipe unlocked at Dark Oak Log VI (6) ([[Dark Oak Log]]). The Bat Crystal ([[Floating Crystals]]) does not regenrate mushrooms. After obtaining one mushroom of each color, they can be grown on Podzol from the [[Farming Island]] or the Garden preset to obtain more. Either 1 [[Enchanted Red Mushroom]] or 1 [[Enchanted Brown Mushroom]] is guaranteed to drop from [[Slug]] Pests on [[The Garden]] and can be used to paste a plot.|
|[[Raw Mutton]]|Dropped by Sheep.|

More can be obtained from the [[Nether Wart Crystal]] on the [[Nether Wart Island]], which requires Netherrack I (1) ([[Netherrack]]) to craft. ▦ Recipe Recipe: [[Seeds]] ×4 + [[Fermented Spider Eye]] → [[Nether Wart]] 3 [[Enchanted Nether Wart]] is a guaranteed drop from [[Beetle]] Pests on [[The Garden]] and can be used to paste a plot.|
|[[Potato]]|1% chance to be dropped by Zombies. They can then be planted on farmland. 3 [[Enchanted Potato]] is a guaranteed drop from [[Locust]] Pests on [[The Garden]] and can be used to paste a plot.|

Pumpkins can be placed in the crafting grid to turn them into pumpkin seeds, which can then be planted. 1 [[Enchanted Pumpkin]] is a guaranteed drop from [[Rat]] Pests on [[The Garden]] and can be used to paste a plot. A Rat Pest is guaranteed to spawn during the Pest tutorial at Garden Level V (5).|

|[[Raw Rabbit]]|Dropped by Rabbits.|

[Mining Collections]
|[[Coal]]|There is a single coal ore at 7, 93, 36 ([[Coordinates]]) on the island that has Jerry. Using 1 Coal to craft an [[Auto Smelter]] allows any Foraging Minion to make Coal. There is a Trade ([[Trades]]) for Coal, which has no unlock requirements, but it doesn't increase the collection. Wood ([[Wood (disambiguation)]]) can be smelted in a furnace to make Charcoal, but it doesn't increase the collection. Coal is also a drop from Lava Fishing.|
|[[Cobblestone]]|Obtained by mining Stone. Can be obtained in large amounts by making a Cobblestone generator using the materials in the chest on the island that has Jerry. There is also a [[Cobblestone Minion]] that starts on the island.|
|[[Diamond]]|Dropped by Charged Creepers. Harvested by Revenant Minions ([[Revenant Minion]]).|
|[[Emerald]]|Obtained by trading with Villagers. Note that Emerald Minions ([[Emerald Minion]]) are 40 times slower on Stranded profiles.|
|[[End Stone]]|Dropped by Zealots.|
|[[Gemstone]]|Some types are dropped by Crystal Hollows Sea Creatures ([[Sea Creatures]]).|
|[[Glowstone Dust]]|Dropped by Witches.|
|[[Gold Ingot]]|Dropped by Redstone Pigmen. Dropped by Golden Ghouls (requires Combat XXIV (24) ([[Combat]])).|
|[[Gravel]]|Obtained by using a TNT explosion to destroy Stone. Both naturally-occurring Stone and manually placed Stone can drop Gravel. Sometimes Flint drops instead of Gravel.|
|[[Hard Stone]]|Dropped by Water Worms ([[Water Worm]]).|
|[[Ice]]|Obtained by using any tool with [[Silk Touch]] to mine Ice, which is formed by letting water freeze in areas where the biome was changed with a Taiga Biome Stick ([[Biome Stick]]) (requires Spruce Log IV (4) ([[Spruce Log]])). Dropped by some Winter Sea Creatures ([[Sea Creatures]]). Obtained by fishing with the [[Ice Rod]], which has a chance to drop from [[Frozen Steve]].|
|[[Iron Ingot]]|Dropped by Iron Golems.|
|[[Lapis Lazuli]]|Dropped by Lapis Zombies.|
|[[Mithril]]|Dropped by Star Sentrys.|
|[[Mycelium]]|Purchased from a Villager Trade for 1 [[Enchanted Emerald]]. The item can be purchased from the following Stranded-only Trade, but it does not count towards the collection. Trade: 1 [[Dirt]], 1 Red Mushroom ([[Mushroom]]), and 1 Brown Mushroom ([[Mushroom]]) → 1 [[Mycelium]] (requires Mushroom II (2) ([[Mushroom]])) The following instructions allow the player to farm [[Mycelium]] and increase the collection. Show Instructions Make a large dirt platform. Either do it in an area that is exposed to sunlight, or light up the surface. Mycelium needs a light level of at least 9 above it to spread to nearby dirt, and the dirt needs a light level of at least 4, so not everything needs to be at light level 9 or higher, but most of it should be. If you want to increase your Mycelium collection, plant a bunch of 2x2 trees on top of the dirt. Trees place dirt blocks under their trunks when they generate, and once they have been turned into Mycelium, these blocks will count towards the Mycelium collection. Add one or more Mycelium blocks for it to spread from. If you don't have any yet, use the Stranded-only Trade to get some. Wait until the platform has finished turning into Mycelium. Use a shovel with [[Silk Touch]] to harvest the Mycelium. If you don't need to increase the collection, you can use a [[Block Zapper]] instead.|
|[[Nether Quartz]]|Dropped by Zombie Miners ([[Abyssal Miner]]) (requires Fishing XXIV (24) ([[Fishing]])). Produced by the [[Voidling Minion]] (requires Enderman Slayer IV (4) ([[Enderman Slayer]])).|
|[[Netherrack]]|Obtained by Lava Fishing.|
|[[Obsidian]]|Obtained by using a pickaxe with a [[Breaking Power]] of at least 4 to mine Obsidian generated by putting a lava source in contact with water. Additional Water Buckets and Lava Buckets are available through Trades. Obsidian can also be mined from the [[Nether Wart Island]] (requires Netherrack I (1) ([[Netherrack]])). Dropped by Obsidian Defenders.|
|[[Red Sand]]|Purchased from a Villager Trade for 1 [[Enchanted Emerald]]. The item can be purchased from the following Stranded-only Trade, but it does not count towards the collection. Trade: 1 [[Sand]] → 1 [[Red Sand]] (requires Sand II (2) ([[Sand]]))|
|[[Redstone Dust]]|Dropped by Redstone Pigmen.|
|[[Sand]]|Obtained from the [[Desert Island]]. Prior to reaching Cactus V (5) ([[Cactus]]), the Desert Island can be obtained from Villagers. Obtained from the [[Pond Island]] (requires Raw Cod IV (4) ([[Raw Cod]])).|
|[[Sulphur]]|The Sulphur collection is not required to unlock the Builder. Purchased from a Villager Trade for 1 [[Enchanted Emerald]]. Obtained by killing Corrupted Sea Creatures. The following instructions allow the player to get larger amounts of [[Sulphur]] and increase the collection. Show Instructions Lava fish to get Corrupted Fragments ([[Corrupted Fragment]]), which are guaranteed to drop from Corrupted ([[Corrupted Mobs]]) Sea Creatures. The following increase the chance to fish up Corrupted Sea Creatures. +0.1% per level from the [[Fishing]] skill. +1% per level from the [[Corruption]] Enchantment, which is obtained by killing corrupted mobs. +0.2% per tier from the Infection Attribute ([[Attributes]]), which has a 1/3 chance to be applied to a [[Magma Rod]] when it is crafted. When you reach Magmafish IV (4) ([[Magmafish]]) (1,000 items), you can craft a [[Magma Rod]] with 10 [[Silver Magmafish]]. Magma Rods require Fishing XXVII (27) ([[Fishing]]) to use. [[Attributes]] on rods can be combined using /attributemenu if you have an active [[Booster Cookie]]. When you reach Magmafish VI (6) ([[Magmafish]]) (15,000 items), you may want to make a Small Lava Fishing Sack ([[Lava Fishing Sack]]) with 8 [[Silver Magmafish]]. Continue once you have 20 Corrupted Fragments ([[Corrupted Fragment]]). Using the instructions for Mycelium above, reach Mycelium VI (6) ([[Mycelium]]) (10,000 items) and get 25,600 total Mycelium. Craft 160 (2 stacks plus 32) [[Enchanted Mycelium]]. Craft a [[Corrupt Soil]] with the 20 Corrupted Fragments and 160 Enchanted Mycelium. Place the Corrupt Soil in a Minion that generates mobs. Corrupt Soil makes the Minion generate [[Corrupted Mobs]], which lets it collect Sulphur and Corrupted Fragments. The amount of Sulphur and Corrupted Fragments depends on the amount of items the Minion collects, so a [[Slime Minion]] is the most efficient option. Once the Minion has collected 20 Corrupted Fragments, get another 160 Enchanted Mycelium and craft another Corrupt Soil. Keep making more Corrupt Soil for as many Minions as you like.|
[Combat Collections]
|[[Blaze Rod]]|Dropped by Blazes. Dropped by Lava Blazes ([[Lava Blaze]]) from Lava Fishing. Getting [[Blaze Powder]] from Lava Fishing increases the Blaze Rod collection, but Blaze Rods cannot be obtained this way.|
|[[Bone]]|Dropped by Skeletons, Wolves, and Old Wolves.|
|[[Chili Pepper]]|Obtained by putting LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]]) [[Inferno Minion Fuel]] into an [[Inferno Minion]]. The following instructions explain how to do so in more detail. Show Instructions These instructions assume that you already have the necessary equipment for slaying Tier I Inferno Demonlords ([[Inferno Demonlord]]). ▦ Show Recipes First, gather the following materials and use them to craft 2 [[Amalgamated Crimsonite]]. Recipe: [[Enchanted Glowstone Dust]] x32/[[Enchanted Glowstone]] x4 + [[Enchanted Mycelium]] x8/[[Enchanted Mycelium Cube]] + [[Enchanted Magma Cream]] x32/[[Whipped Magma Cream]] x4 + [[Enchanted Blaze Powder]] x32/[[Enchanted Blaze Rod]] x4 + [[Enchanted Red Sand]] x8/[[Enchanted Red Sand Cube]] + [[Enchanted Nether Wart]] x32/[[Mutant Nether Wart]] x4 → [[Amalgamated Crimsonite]] x2/[[Amalgamated Crimsonite]] x40 8 [[Enchanted Mycelium]] 8 [[Enchanted Red Sand]] 32 [[Enchanted Glowstone Dust]] 32 [[Enchanted Magma Cream]] 32 [[Enchanted Blaze Powder]] 32 [[Enchanted Nether Wart]] Slay Tier I Inferno Demonlords ([[Inferno Demonlord]]) until you have 256 (4 stacks) [[Derelict Ashe]]. Craft 2 [[Molten Powder]] using the [[Amalgamated Crimsonite]] and [[Derelict Ashe]]. Recipe: [[Derelict Ashe]] x32 ×4 + [[Amalgamated Crimsonite]] → [[Molten Powder]] Craft a [[Firedust Dagger]] using 30 [[Silver Magmafish]], 1 [[Molten Powder]], and a Stick. Recipe: [[Silver Magmafish]] x30 + [[Molten Powder]] + [[Stick]] → [[Firedust Dagger]] Craft a [[Twilight Dagger]] using 5 [[Enchanted Sulphur]], 1 [[Molten Powder]], and a Stick. Recipe: [[Enchanted Sulphur]] x5 + [[Molten Powder]] + [[Stick]] → [[Twilight Dagger]] Using the daggers, slay Tier II Inferno Demonlords ([[Inferno Demonlord]]) until you have 80 (1 stack plus 16) [[Derelict Ashe]]. Craft a [[Blaze Minion]] using 80 Blaze Rods ([[Blaze Rod]]) and a Wooden Sword. Recipe: [[Blaze Rod]] x10 ×8 + [[Wooden Sword]] → [[Blaze Minion]] Craft an [[Inferno Minion]] using the [[Blaze Minion]] and 80 [[Derelict Ashe]]. Recipe: [[Derelict Ashe]] x10 ×8 + [[Blaze Minion]] → [[Inferno Minion]] Keep slaying Tier II Inferno Demonlords ([[Inferno Demonlord]]). As you get more [[Derelict Ashe]], make more Inferno Minions. Except for Tier I, all tiers of the [[Inferno Demonlord]] have a chance to drop [[Distillates]]. The Tier II boss has a 21.1% chance to drop 2 matching Distillates. The Tier III boss has a 20.3% chance to drop a set of 6 to 10 matching Distillates. The Tier IV boss has a 19.2% chance to drop a set of 16 to 24 matching Distillates. Continue once you have 13,824 (216 stacks) [[Crude Gabagool]] from the Inferno Minions and 6 matching Distillates. Craft 604 (9 stacks plus 28) [[Sulphuric Coal]] using the following materials. You only need 601, but they are crafted 4 at a time. Recipe: [[Chili Pepper]] ×4 + [[Enchanted Coal]] x4 ×4 + [[Enchanted Sulphur]] → [[Sulphuric Coal]] x4/[[Sulphuric Coal]] x12 151 (2 stacks plus 23) [[Enchanted Sulphur]], which is equivalent to 24,160 (377 stacks plus 32) [[Sulphur]]. 2,416 (37 stacks plus 48) [[Enchanted Coal]], which would take 62 days to get using a single Tier XI [[Coal Minion]]. Craft 576 (9 stacks) [[Fuel Gabagool]] using 13,824 [[Crude Gabagool]] and 576 [[Sulphuric Coal]]. Recipe: [[Crude Gabagool]] x3/[[Sulphuric Coal]] ×8 + [[Sulphuric Coal]]/[[Very Crude Gabagool]] → [[Fuel Gabagool]]/[[Fuel Gabagool]] x8 Craft 24 [[Heavy Gabagool]] using 576 [[Fuel Gabagool]] and 24 [[Sulphuric Coal]]. Recipe: [[Fuel Gabagool]] x3 ×8 + [[Sulphuric Coal]] → [[Heavy Gabagool]] Craft a [[Hypergolic Gabagool]] using 24 [[Heavy Gabagool]] and 1 [[Sulphuric Coal]]. Recipe: [[Heavy Gabagool]] x3 ×4 + [[Sulphuric Coal]] → [[Hypergolic Gabagool]] Buy 2 Inferno Fuel Blocks ([[Inferno Fuel Block]]) from the [[Bits Shop]] for 75 [[Bits]] each. Using the Inferno Fuel Blocks ([[Inferno Fuel Block]]), [[Hypergolic Gabagool]], and 6 matching [[Distillates]], craft a LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]]) [[Inferno Minion Fuel]]. Recipe: [[Distillates]] ×6 + [[Inferno Fuel Block]] ×2 + [[Hypergolic Gabagool]] → [[Inferno Minion Fuel]] Put the LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]]) [[Inferno Minion Fuel]] into one of your Inferno Minions ([[Inferno Minion]]). When using LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]]) [[Inferno Minion Fuel]], Inferno Minions have a 1/156 chance per harvest to generate a [[Chili Pepper]]. On average, they will generate 10.05 Chili Peppers ([[Chili Pepper]]) from each LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]]) [[Inferno Minion Fuel]], which each last 24 hours. There is a 0.0042% chance that they won't generate any Chili Peppers ([[Chili Pepper]]). If that somehow happens, you have to make another LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]]) [[Inferno Minion Fuel]].|

## Villagers
Villagers can be obtained from curing Zombie Villagers. Players can sell some items to Villagers in exchange for [[Emerald|Emeralds]] and [[Enchanted Emerald|Enchanted Emeralds]]. Players can then exchange these Emeralds and Enchanted Emeralds for [[Pets]], [[Dark Auction]] items, and items from [[Shops|Merchant NPCs]], among other things.
### Curing Zombie Villagers
Zombie Villagers can be cured by being fed a [[Golden Apple]] while under the [[Weakness Potion|Weakness]] effect, just like in vanilla Minecraft. Golden Apples can be obtained as a [[Rarity|RARE]] drop from fishing or by crafting with gold from killing [[Redstone Pigman]]. The Weakness effect can be obtained by having a Witch throw a Splash Potion of Weakness onto the Zombie Villager or by [[Potions|brewing]] a Splash [[Weakness Potion]].
### Villager Leveling
Villagers level up after a set number of Emeralds are traded. When a Villager levels up, it unlocks more trades. After a level is reached, the counter resets. Enchanted Emeralds count as 160 Emeralds for Villager leveling.
|Level|Emeralds Needed|Cumulative|
|-|-|-|
|1|(none)|0|
|2|50|50|
|3|400|450|
|4|1050|1500|
|5|1500|3000|
### Villager Trades
The list of Villager trades is shown below. The first table shows items that the player can give to a Villager for 1 Emerald, and the second table shows items that the player can buy with Emeralds or Enchanted Emeralds.

### Notes on Villagers
- Villagers do not despawn.
- Villagers cannot be bred.
- The prices offered by a single Villager never change.
- Punching or otherwise harming a Villager does not affect the prices.
- The prices offered by different Villagers when trading items for Emeralds can differ slightly between Villagers, but prices when trading Emeralds for other items are always the same for that item.
- Hostile creatures do not attack Villagers.
- If there are at least six Villagers on the island, Iron Golems will spawn, allowing the player to unlock the [[Iron Ingot]] collection.
- Villagers move very quickly once cured, so it is advised to cure Zombie Villagers after they are trapped.
- Villagers tend to clip through walls, so it is best to use a solid block at their feet and a non-solid block at head level. Using a solid block at head level can suffocate them.
- Do not place a ceiling directly above them since this can cause mobs to clip through the floor.
- The first Villager cured on each profile is guaranteed to have the [[Desert Island]] trade. If this Villager dies, the next does not have the same guarantee.
- A bug causes Villagers to occasionally load as vanilla villagers. This applies to all Villagers when it happens and can be fixed by reloading the island (go to the lobby, wait at least five seconds, and rejoin). Trading with vanilla villagers does not count towards collections.
## Trades Menu
The Stranded [[Trades]] menu that can be accessed through /trade has a few changes. The [[Portals|Portal to the Hub]] Trade is removed and there are a few additional trades exclusive to Stranded. The regular water and lava bucket trades are still available upon meeting their normal requirements.

## Events
Players on Stranded profiles can participate in most [[Events]], but some Events do not affect Stranded profiles.
### Recurring Events
- [[Jacob's Farming Contest]]: ✔ Jacob and Anita can be found in the Island NPCs menu and in [[The Garden]].
- [[Dark Auction]]: ✕ Dark Auctions are not available, but many items from the Dark Auction are obtainable by trading with Villagers.
- [[New Year Celebration]]: ✔ The [[Baker]] can be found in the Island NPCs menu.
- [[Traveling Zoo]]: ✔ [[Oringo]] can be found in the Island NPCs menu. Spawning Oringo requires 6 unique pets.
- [[Hoppity's Hunt]]: ✔ [[Hoppity]] can be found in the Island NPCs menu.
- [[Spooky Festival]]: (partial)
  - Spooky Mobs can spawn on the Private Island on Stranded profiles.
  - Spooky Sea Creatures can also be fished up.
  - The [[Headless Horseman]] can be spawned on the private island.
  - The [[Fear Mongerer]] can be found in the Island NPCs menu. Spawning The Fear Mongerer requires [[Combat|Combat V (5)]].
  - Leaderboard rewards can still be obtained from the Events menu in the [[SkyBlock Menu]].
    - Note: score is capped at 5,000 [[Spooky Festival|points]] for Stranded Profiles
  - The [[Bat Firework]] cannot be purchased. Instead, [[Bat Piñata|Bat Piñatas]] will occasionally spawn.
  - [[Crazy Witch|Crazy Witches]] fully heal very frequently, but it is barely possible to kill them. For example, they can be killed by trapping them in lava, using a [[Starlight Wand]], and hitting them frequently.
- [[Season of Jerry]] and other [[Jerry's Workshop|Winter Island]] features: (partial)
  - The [[Jerry's Workshop|Winter Island]] cannot be visited, but Stranded players can fish up [[Sea Creatures|Winter Sea Creatures]] on the Private Island any time during Late Winter.
  - Fishing up Winter Sea Creatures is the only way to obtain Gifts since Stranded players cannot travel to the [[Jerry's Workshop|Winter Island]] to participate in the [[Defend Jerry's Workshop]] event.
  - Players can open any type of [[Gifts|Gift]] by placing it on the ground and opening it as usual, even on a Solo profile.
  - On [[Co-op]] profiles, giving the gift to a Co-op member instead of oneself will give the reward to both players, resulting in essentially double drops.
  - Rewards from the [[Chicken Race]] can be bought from Villagers.
- [[Mayor Election]]: ✕
  - Elections cannot be voted on by players on Stranded profiles.
  - Players on Stranded profiles cannot interact with elected mayors.
  - Many mayor perks still apply to Stranded profiles, so it is still beneficial to vote on the election from a non-Stranded profile.
  - For more information on which mayor perks apply to Stranded, see § Mayors below.
### Mayor Events
- [[Mythological Ritual]] ([[Diana]]): ✕ Unavailable
- [[Mining Fiesta]] ([[Cole]]/[[Foxy]]): ✕ Unavailable
- [[Fishing Festival]] ([[Marina]]/[[Foxy]]): ✔ Available
### Other Events
- [[Bingo|Ⓑ Bingo]]: The Bingo interface can be opened via the /bingo command, allowing access to the Bingo profile, card, and shop. Items cannot be transferred from a Bingo profile to a Stranded profile.
- ✕ [[Century Cakes|Century Cake]]: [[Simon]] was not in the Island NPCs menu during the Year 200 event, so he is unlikely to appear for future century events.
- ✔ [[SkyBlock Anniversary]]: During the 3rd to 6th [[SkyBlock Anniversary]] events, [[Simon]] was in the Island NPCs Menu, and Stranded players were able to claim the Party Hat.
  - ✕ [[Shiny Pig|Shiny Pigs]]: The [[Piggy|Pig Shop]] was available in the Island NPCs Menu during the 3rd to 5th SkyBlock Anniversary events. However, since this was moved to Year of the Pig, it does not function properly. During Year of the Pig, nothing appears on Stranded. During SkyBlock Anniversaries, Shiny Pigs spawn but the Pig Shop is unavailable.
## Mayors
While [[Mayor Election|mayors]] cannot be voted on or spoken with on Stranded profiles, many perks still apply.
- [[Aatrox]]: ✔ All perks apply as usual.
- [[Cole]]: (partial) Since his Mining XP Buff perk and the [[Mining Fiesta]] event only work on public islands, and that there is no access to [[The Forge]] so Molten Forge has no purpose, the only one of his perks that affects Stranded is the Prospection perk, which increases the speed of Mining Minions by 25%.
- [[Diana]]: (partial) The [[Mythological Ritual]] event is only available on the Hub Island. The [[Griffin Pet]] cannot be obtained because the mayors cannot be spoken with on Stranded. Her other perks, Pet XP Buff, Lucky! and Sharing is Caring, apply as usual.
- [[Diaz]]: (partial) Because you can't access Shen's Auction or Stonks Auction on Stranded profiles, her only perk that applies is Shopping Spree.
- [[Finnegan]]: (partial) All perks apply as usual, except for some mechanics of the [[Harvest Feast|Grand Feast]] as you cannot visit [[Feast Baker Scott]] to trade in [[Kernels]] for items as he is located on the hub island.
- [[Foxy]]: (partial) Because Carnivals can't be accessed in Stranded, the only perk that applies to Stranded profiles is her Extra Events if it happens to be Fishing Festival or Spooky Festival and Sweet Benevolence applies as usual[Confirm].
- [[Marina]]: ✔ The Fishing XP Buff perk includes Stranded islands, and her other perks apply as usual.
- [[Paul]]: ✕ Since dungeons are not available, none of Paul's perks apply to Stranded profiles.
### Special Candidates
- [[Derpy]]: (partial) All perks apply as usual, except the QUAD TAXES!!! perk does not affect Stranded because Stranded players cannot access the Auction House and Bazaar* anyway. (*Except for booster cookies.)
- [[Candidate Jerry|Jerry]]: ✔ All perks apply as usual.
- [[Scorpius]]: ✕ Since mayors cannot be spoken with on Stranded, his Bribe perk does not apply. Since the Dark Auction cannot be accessed, his only other perk, Darker Auctions, also does not apply.
### One-Time Candidates
Many items that were only obtainable while the mayor was [[Dante]] or [[Resistance Leader|Technoblade]] can be obtained by trading with Villagers. During the 3rd SkyBlock Anniversary event, the [[Piggy|Pig Shop]] was available in the Island NPCs Menu, and [[Shiny Pig|Shiny Pigs]] spawned on the island during part of the event.
## Fishing
A few changes were made to [[Fishing]] for Stranded profiles only.
- The debuff to [[Sea Creature Chance]] on the Private Island is removed.
- Fishing bobbers can deal damage to mobs.
- [[Sea Creatures]] that usually only spawn in some specific locations can be fished up on the Private Island.
- The [[Abyssal Miner]] drops [[Nether Quartz]].
- [[Water Worm|Water Worms]] drop [[Hard Stone]].
- Name Tags are a GREAT CATCH! from Fishing, and they can be used to prevent mobs from despawning (up to 5 are able to be used).
- The Fishing [[Fairy Souls]] can be collected like normal, but there is no way to trade them in for buffs, so the only use for Fairy Souls is the Full Set Bonus of [[Fairy Armor]].
- Changing the Biome with a [[Biome Stick|Deep Ocean Biome Stick]] grants +5 [[Sea Creature Chance]] in that area.
### Lava Fishing
Lava Fishing is possible by summoning [[Odger]] from the Island NPCs Menu and getting a [[Starter Lava Rod]].
- The chance for Sea Creatures to be [[Corrupted Mobs|Corrupted]] when Lava Fishing increases by 0.1% per [[Fishing]] level.
- [[Magma Rod|Magma Rods]] can be crafted upon reaching [[Magmafish|Magmafish IV (4)]] (1000 [[Magmafish]]) and used upon reaching [[Fishing|Fishing XXVII (27)]] .
- Lava Fishing is the only way to increase the [[Netherrack]] and [[Magmafish]] collections.
### Trophy Fishing
The requirements for catching [[Trophy Fish]] are different on Stranded profiles. Show/Hide Trophy Fish List

|Item|Requirement|Chance|Changed (Whether or not the method to obtain the Trophy Fish is different on Stranded than it is in other gamemodes)|
|-|-|-|-|
|Blobfish|No requirement.|15%|✕|
|Flyfish|The bobber must be at least 8 blocks below the player.|8%|✕|
|Golden Fish|Fishing for 15+ minutes. See Trophy Fishes § Golden Fish ([[Trophy Fish]]).||✕|
|Gusher|The biome must be changed with a Nether Biome Stick ([[Biome Stick]]).|20%|✔|
|Karate Fish|The player must punch the air and/or blocks at least 20 times after casting.|1%|✔|
|Lavahorse|No requirement.|4%|✕|
|Mana Ray|The player must have at least 1200 [[Mana]].|4%|✕|
|Moldfin|There must be at least 8 Mycelium blocks within 5 blocks of the bobber.|2%|✔|
|Skeleton Fish|There must be a Skeleton within 8 blocks of the bobber.|2%|✔|
|Slugfish|At least 20 seconds must pass between casting the rod and reeling it in.|10%|✕|
|Soul Fish|There must be at least 8 [[Soul Sand]] blocks within 5 blocks of the bobber.|2%|✔|
|Steaming-Hot Flounder|It must be raining, which can be controlled with a Weather Stick ([[Weather Wand]]).|20%|✔|
