|Volcanic Stonefish|There must be a [[Hard Stone Minion]] within 8 blocks of the bobber.|3%|✔|
|Obfuscated 1|The player must use [[Corrupted Bait]].|25%|✕|
|Obfuscated 2|The player must use Obfuscated 1 as bait.|20%|✕|
|Obfuscated 3|The player must use Obfuscated 2 as bait.|10%|✕|
Note: Obfuscated 1 Trophy Fish can also be obtained by killing [[Corrupted Mobs|Corrupted]] Sea Creatures, so using a [[Totem of Corruption]] on Sea Creatures to get a lot of Obfuscated 1 will help speed up the process when trying to get Obfuscated 2 and Obfuscated 3. Getting Obfuscated 1 from Corrupted Sea Creatures doesn't count for [[Odger]]'s quest, so this method should only be used once all tiers of Obfuscated 1 have been obtained using [[Corrupted Bait]].
### Sea Guardians
- In addition to being able to be fished up, [[Sea Guardian|Sea Guardians]] used to spawn naturally in water under the following conditions:
  - The biome in the area must have been changed with a [[Biome Stick|Deep Ocean Biome Stick]].
  - The water must be at least 3 blocks deep, and flowing water does count.
  - They spawn more frequently when there are more viable spots for them to spawn, including vertically. For example, they can spawn in a 2 wide channel, but they would spawn extremely slowly.
  - They will not spawn if there are already six of them on the island.
- They have the same stats and drops as the normal variant that can be fished up.
- [[Lily Pad|Lily Pads]] obtained from killing these Sea Guardians do count towards the collection.
- Unlike in vanilla Minecraft, Sea Guardians can drown if they are underwater for long enough. However, it takes a while due to their high health.
  - Because of this, they will naturally float to the top of the water.
- Killing these Sea Guardians does not count towards the [[Dolphin Pet]] milestones.
## Jerry's Tips
Tips can be bought from [[Jerry]] for 1,000 [[Coins]] each. These tips are hints for Stranded-specific mechanics. For example, the tip "In my opinion, Magma Cubes are just fiery slimes." is a hint for the mechanic where [[Magma Cube|Magma Cubes]] can be spawned by setting [[Slime (disambiguation)|Slimes]] on fire. Upon purchasing a tip from Jerry, the player will receive one of the following messages in chat:
- Villagers tend to have sweet trades!
- Did you know Slimes can spawn near the void?
- Cocoa Beans only grow on one type of tree!
- Shiny creatures can drop shiny things!
- Rabbits can spawn in different biomes!
- I've heard that scary monsters can attack your island sometime in Autumn.
- The only way to obtain Emeralds is to trade for them!
- Pigmen are great at hoarding wealth.
- This place desperately needs more Iron Golems!
- In my opinion, Magma Cubes are just fiery slimes.
- Villagers tend to have cool trades!
## The Garden
[[The Garden]] is generally the same as it is for other profiles, with the following differences.
- Talking to visitors is not required to unlock them. Garden Level requirements are unchanged.
- When pasting a plot, crops cannot be purchased from the [[Bazaar]].
- The [[Fast Travel]] button in the [[SkyBlock Menu]] is replaced with a button that takes the player straight from their island to the Garden, or vice versa.
- [[Tia the Fairy]]'s dialogue as a Garden visitor on Stranded profiles is changed to It's a shame you can't visit public islands. If you ever do, I have a quest for you.
## Trivia
- There is a limit of 1,000 [[Hub Villagers|Villagers]] on the player's [[Private Island]].

# Terminal
Aliases: Terminals
Categories: Confirmations needed
Terminals are devices in [[Goldor]]'s factory which control gates in the third phase. They contain simple tasks players must complete in order to activate them.
## Mechanics
Terminals appear in [[The Catacombs - Floor VII]] in [[Goldor]]'s arena. Several terminals will appear around the arena, as well as one Device: all terminals and devices, alongside levers at the end of the section, must be activated for the gates to open and eventually allow access to the Core Entrance, where [[Goldor]] can be damaged.
Terminals contain simple tasks for players to complete. Once the task is completed, the terminal is activated; the team is alerted of its completion as well as the name of the player who completed it. When all terminals in the gate are activated and the two levers are flipped, the gate will begin to open.
If a terminal's task is failed, the terminal will reset.
## List of Terminals
|Name|Gate|Description|
|-|-|-|
|Color|All|27 items will appear in a chest GUI. The GUI title will instruct players to click on all items of a specific color. Players must click on each item of the given color to complete the terminal.|
|Order|All|10 stacks of panes will appear in a chest GUI, in stacks of 1 through 10. Players must click on them in ascending order to complete the terminal.|
|Name|All|27 items will appear in a chest GUI. The GUI title will instruct players to click on all items beginning with a specific letter. Players must click on each item beginning with the letter to complete the terminal.|
|Melody|All|A green pane will move back and forth: players must wait for the green pane to align with two purple panes before pressing a button to lock in the position. This must be repeated 3 times: locking in the incorrect position causes the green pane to wait 2s[Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)] before continuing to move.|
|Panes|All|15 red and green panes will appear in a chest GUI. Clicking a pane toggles it between green and red. Players must turn all the red panes to green.|
## List of Devices
|Name|Gate|Description|
|-|-|-|
|Simon Says|1|A 4x4 grid of obsidian will appear. Clicking the start button will briefly show a series of sea lanterns which replace the obsidian. After that, 16 buttons will appear corresponding to each obsidian block. Players must push the buttons in order according to the order in which the obsidian was replaced. This device has 5 stages, each one adding 1 more sea lantern.|
|Lights|2|A 5x4 grid of lamps will appear. Flicking a lever will activate its lamp and the 4 directly adjacent lamps. Players must activate all 20 lamps.|
|Arrow Path|3|A grid of item frames with arrows will appear. Players must make each arrow point in the correct direction to form a path from the green wool to the red wool. If the arrow is rotated correctly, a sea lantern will appear underneath it.|
|Sharpshooter|4|A 3x3 grid of purple glass will appear. Players must stand on a gold pressure plate to start the device. 1 purple glass will turn into an emerald block; players must shoot it with an arrow. This will change the location of the emerald block. After enough times, the device will be completed.|
## Trivia
- The [[Puzzle Cube]] can be used to simulate a Terminal.
- Terminals are a reference to tasks in Among Us. Some terminals are similar in content to Among Us tasks.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Aug 25, 2026|Reduced the amount of rows on Melody from 4 → 3.|
|Aug 25, 2026|Reduced the amount of numbers on Numbers from 14 → 10|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Nov 17, 2020|0.9.13|Added Terminals.|
|Oct 6, 2026|0.27.2|Reduced the amount of rows on Melody from 4 → 3.|
|Oct 6, 2026|0.27.2|Reduced the amount of numbers on Numbers from 14 → 10|

# Torrhus Beacon
Categories: Confirmations needed, Galatea
[Torrhus Beacon]
The Torrhus Beacon is a beacon in the [[Torrhus Canyon]] that, once tuned properly, grants increased [[Helix Fortune]] depending on how many stacks it has and its signal strength.
## Overview
### Frequency Tuning
To tune the Torrhus Beacon, one must correctly match the Color, Speed, and Pitch of a beat (telegraphed by colored glass panes). Successfully tuning the beacon will grant +1 Stack to all players on the island for 20 minutes. Each Stack grants +5 [[Fig Fortune]] and +5 [[Mangrove Fortune]]. Players by default can only get bonuses from 5 stacks.
There is also a 10% chance to obtain a [[Beaconmite Shard]], which when syphoned, grants the [[Attributes|Beacon Zealot]] attribute.
### Increasing Signal Strength
#### Beacon Zealot
The [[Attributes|Beacon Zealot]] attribute, obtained from the [[Beaconmite Shard]] increases the stats granted by the beacon to the player by 10%->100%. This percentage bonus is applied after the raw stat bonus from Signal Enhancers. Additionally it also grants a 0.1%->1% chance to obtain [[Signal Enhancer|Signal Enhancers]] from [[Tree Gifts]].
#### Signal Enhancers
A [[Signal Enhancer]] can be used to increase the Signal Strength of the Torrhus Beacon. This can be done a maximum of 10 times. Before obtaining the bonus, players must first solve a harder version of the Frequency Tuning minigame. The [[Signal Enhancer]] is only consumed after being solved. Each level of Signal Strength grants an additional +0.5 [[Fig Fortune]] and +0.5 [[Mangrove Fortune]] per Stack from the Torrhus Beacon. Every other[Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)] consumed Signal Enhancer also increases the number of stacks players can be affected by by 1 up to a total maximum of 10.
With [[Attributes|Beacon Zealot X]] and 10 consumed [[Signal Enhancer|Signal Enhancers]], players can get up to 200 [[Fig Fortune]] and 200 [[Mangrove Fortune]] from the Torrhus Beacon, compared to the default maximum of 25 of each.
## UI
Torrhus Beacon/UI
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Jul 10, 2026|Added Torrhus Beacon.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Aug 4, 2026|0.27|Added Torrhus Beacon.|

# Year of the Witch
Aliases: Aerodynamic Potpourri, Bone Broth, Brittle Powder, Console Panel, Cremated Cereal, Crunchy Bug, Dirty Fingernails, Dusty Bisque, Effervescent Bouillon, Electronic Elixir, Festering Fly, Flaming Hot Chilli, Frog Legs, Frogspawn Chowder, Funny Bone, Gelatinous Gloop, Gemstone Goulash, Glaciated Brains, Hopefully-not-human Hodgepodge, Ingredient, Insect Casserole, Jawbreaker, Knockoff Glacite Gumbo, Leafy Lentil Soup, Mayfly Stew, Never-Melt Ice, Pixie Dust, Rotted Leaf, Ruffled Feather, Shield Dust, Smelly Jelly, Smoldering Embers, Spell Soot, Spellweaver's Soup, Spider Egg, Spider Eye Soup, Straight Ice Slushie, Void Ooze, Void Porridge, Warmed Flakes, Waterbourne Lice, Waterlice Liquor, Witch Year, Witch year, Witches Stew, Withering Purée, YotW
Categories: Confirmations needed, Events, Year of the Witch
Year of the Witch
:
Countdown:
Dates and times based on browser time.
The Year of the Witch is an [[Events|Event]] that happens every 12 [[Time Systems|SkyBlock Years]].
## Mechanics
During the Year of the Witch, [[Agnes]] and [[Alcina]] appear in the [[Village]] and slain [[Mob|Mobs]] drop Ingredients.
### Ingredients
During the Year of the Witch, mobs drop Ingredients depending on [[Mob Types|Mob Type]]. Only the player who deals the killing blow to a mob rolls for ingredients. These chances are not affected by [[Looting]] or [[Chance]], only the [[Witch Pet]]'s Toil and Trouble perk and the [[Attributes|Witch's Fortune]] attribute from the [[Sea Witch Shard]].
Ingredients can be obtained in the [[Critter Safari]], and will be put into the Player's Safari Stash. They can be retreieved with the /pickupsafaristash command. The automatic [[Loot Sharing|Loot Share]] in the [[Critter Safari]] does not apply to Ingredients.[Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)]

## Trivia
- [[Year of the Witch|Never-Melt Ice]], item rarely dropped from [[Frozen (Mob Type)|Frozen]] mobs, shares its name with an equipable item from Pokémon, boosting the power of all Ice Moves.
  - [[Year of the Witch|Shield Dust]] shares the name of a special ability from the same series.
- Only [[Timid]], [[Pest (Mob Type)|Pest]], [[Spooky]] and [[Mythological]] dont has own Ingredient and Witch Stew.
  - [[Spooky]] and [[Mythological]]'s Ingredient posibly not added because mobs found only in time-gated event such as [[Spooky Festival]] and [[Mythological Ritual]]
  - On the other hand, [[Timid]] and [[Pest (Mob Type)|Pest]] mobs can be found any time, but dont has own Ingredient.
    - Since [[Pest (Mob Type)|Pest]] mobs affects by [[Overbloom]] instead of [[Magic Find]], theoreticaly Witch Stew will gain +1 [[Overbloom]] on [[Pest (Mob Type)|Pest]] instead.
## Tips
- [[Frozen (Mob Type)|Frozen]] Mobs exist outside of [[Jerry's Workshop]] and can be found at any time in the Icy biome of the [[Critter Safari]].

# Event Square
Categories: Conjectural articles, Locations
This article's name is conjectural. An official name is yet to be given and the name of this page may change at any time.
[Event Square]
Type: Location ([[Locations]])
The Event Square is a location to the left of the [[Community Center]] where several events in the [[Hub Island]] are held.
## Overview
The Event Square consists of a fountain in the middle of a plaza connecting the [[Village]] with a portal to the [[Dungeon Hub]]. A fountain composed of Chiseled Tuff and Chiseled Tuff Bricks can be found in the middle.
During its respective events, the Event Square changes appearance:
- While the [[Traveling Zoo]] is active, three carts surround the outer perimeter of the Event Square. [[Oringo]] stands in front of the middle cart.
- While the [[Spooky Festival]] is active, a giant jack-o'-lantern is placed in the back of the Event Square. The [[Fear Mongerer]] stands in front of the giant jack-o'-lantern, behind a desk.
- While the [[Season of Jerry]] is active and [[Jerry's Workshop]] is active, a giant Christmas tree can be found in the back of the Event Square, planted on top of two huts. Large, wrapped gifts of assorted colors and sizes can be found. [[Jerry]] stands in the middle of the center hut.
  - While the [[New Year Celebration]] is active, the [[Baker]] stands in the leftmost hut, where cakes can be found.
## Gallery
- The Traveling Zoo The [[Traveling Zoo]]
- The Event Square while the Spooky Festival is active The Event Square while the [[Spooky Festival]] is active
- The Event Square while the Season of Jerry is active The Event Square while the [[Season of Jerry]] is active
Linked from: [[Events]], [[Village]]

# Golden Dante Statue
Aliases: Dante Statue
Categories: Conjectural articles, Historical content, Locations, The Uprising
This article's name is conjectural. An official name is yet to be given and the name of this page may change at any time.
Golden Dante Statue was significantly altered or removed after an update. This article is retained for historical purposes.
[Golden Dante Statue]
Type: Structure
Fairy Souls: 1
Location: [[Village]]
Coordinates: -17 70 -110
The Golden Dante Statue was a structure located in the [[Village]] at [[Coordinates|-17, 70, -110]] that appeared when [[Dante]] got elected. It had no use other than being decorative.
## Overview
When [[Dante]] got elected, a golden statue of him replaced the well to the right of the [[Community Center]]. The [[Fairy Souls|Fairy Soul]] originally in the well was still accessible through some vines behind the golden statue on the pedestal. During the [[Dante|Dante fight]], the statue was destroyed and, once Dante was defeated and new [[Mayor Election|Mayors]] were chosen, it was replaced by [[Blood God Shrine]].
## Trivia
- Although the statue was guarded by four [[Goons]], it was still accessible without a [[Happy Mask]] or a [[Dante's Talisman]].
- The statue was likely a reference to real-life statues of dictators.
- After the resistance won, it was replaced with the [[Blood God Shrine]].
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Apr 4, 2021|Added Golden Dante Statue|
|Apr 16, 2021|The statue got replaced by the [[Blood God Shrine]].|
Linked from: [[Blood God Shrine]], [[Dante]], [[Gold (disambiguation)]], [[Goons]]

# Placeable Backpack
Categories: Conjectural articles, Glitches, Uncommon items
This article's name is conjectural. An official name is yet to be given and the name of this page may change at any time.
Placeable Backpacks, called Small Backpacks in game, are [[Glitched Items|glitched]], unobtainable [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] placeable items.
## Appearance
Placeable Backpacks look exactly the same as purple dyed [[Backpack|Small Backpacks]], except they lack the lore. Like the community given name suggests, these backpacks can be placed and picked up on private islands. These backpacks cannot store items, and can be put inside other backpacks. After the duplication exploit in 2021, more than 1000 of them are known to exist. They can be auctioned.
## Obtaining
Placeable Backpacks are considered glitched items. They cannot be obtained anymore, the only way to obtain them now is to purchase from the [[Auction House]].
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Unknown Date|Players started exploiting Placeable Backpack.|

# Backpack Skins
Aliases: Backpack Skin, Chest Skins
Categories: Cosmetics
[Backpack Skins]
The Enderpack Backpack Skin, which can be applied to Backpacks to change their appearance.
Backpack Skins are cosmetic items that are able to be applied to [[Backpack|Backpacks]] to change their appearance. They can be purchased from [[Fire Sale|Fire Sales]] for [[SkyBlock Gems]].
A Backpack Skin can be added on a [[Backpack]] by combining the desired Skin with any Backpack in an [[Anvil]].

Tooltip: A bag with 9 slots which can be placed in your Storage Menu to store additional items.; UNCOMMON
Backpacks are unlocked in the [[Leather]] collection, and can be interacted with to open and store items. It can be opened by going to [[Storage]] in your SkyBlock Menu and left clicking one of the backpacks you have stored.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|Small Backpack ([[Backpack]])|Leather III (3) ([[Leather]])|80x [[Leather]]|Recipe: [[Leather]] x10 ×8 → [[Backpack]]|
|Medium Backpack ([[Backpack]])|Leather VI (6) ([[Leather]])|1x Small Backpack ([[Backpack]]) 512x [[Leather]]|Recipe: [[Leather]] x64 ×8 + [[Backpack]] → [[Backpack]]|
|Large Backpack ([[Backpack]])|Leather IX (9) ([[Leather]])|1x Medium Backpack ([[Backpack]]) 48x [[Enchanted Leather]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Leather]] x6 ×8 + [[Backpack]] → [[Backpack]]|
|Greater Backpack ([[Backpack]])|Leather XI (11) ([[Leather]])|1x Large Backpack ([[Backpack]]) 96x (1 stack plus 32 (96 in total)) [[Enchanted Leather]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Leather]] x12 ×8 + [[Backpack]] → [[Backpack]]|
|Jumbo Backpack ([[Backpack]])|None|1x [[Jumbo Backpack Upgrade]] 1x Greater Backpack ([[Backpack]])|Recipe: [[Jumbo Backpack Upgrade]] + [[Backpack]] → [[Backpack]]|
## Usage
Backpacks can be used to store items. They can be opened by going to [[Storage]] in your SkyBlock Menu and left clicking one of the backpacks that's been stored there.
The amount of space that a backpack has is based on its tier:
|Tier|Amount of Slots|
|-|-|
|Small Backpack ([[Backpack]])|9|
|Medium Backpack ([[Backpack]])|18|
|Large Backpack ([[Backpack]])|27|
|Greater Backpack ([[Backpack]])|36|
|Jumbo Backpack ([[Backpack]])|45|
## Interface
Tip: click on certain slots to change the UI or go to another article!
## Dyeing
Backpacks can be be crafted with 8 of any color [[Dyes|Dye]]. Dyed backpacks will keep their color when upgraded.
If you place a dyed backpack to your storage, then remove it back, the backpack will turn into its default texture.
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|

## Trivia
- Backpacks were well known for being problematic and often used for duping items. This caused them to be disabled several times. The longest they've ever been disabled is from April 5, 2021 to April 20, 2021. On April 20, Hypixel pushed an update that made backpacks inaccessible outside the storage menu, requiring players to place them into a Backpack Slot to access the items.
  - Due to the Backpack revamp, all legacy backpacks have been made obsolete. Legacy Backpacks that still have items inside them can still be placed into a backpack slot to access the items.
- Backpacks cannot be placed into the [[Ender Chest]], though if a player had a backpack in there prior to April 20, 2021 it will remain there.
- Backpacks can be placed inside the [[Personal Vault]]. However, a backpack must have no items inside it to be taken out the Storage Menu
- It would take 414,720 (6,480 stacks) [[Leather]] and 18 [[Jumbo Backpack Upgrade|Jumbo Backpack Upgrades]] to fill up all 18 backpack slots with [[Backpack|Jumbo Backpacks]].
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jun 11, 2019|0.1|Added Backpack.|
|Jun 26, 2019|0.4|Backpacks now keep stored items upon upgrading.|
|Jul 9, 2019|0.6|Backpacks with items inside are no longer able to be auctioned.|
|Sep 11, 2019|0.7.1|Added Greater Backpack|
|Sep 11, 2019|0.7.1|Added the ability to dye backpacks.|
|Feb 8, 2020|Fixed an exploit where right-clicking with a backpack in your hand without a helmet on would duplicate the backpack onto the user's head.|
|Feb 21, 2020|0.7.6|Added drop protection for small and medium backpacks.|
|Feb 21, 2020|0.7.6|Backpacks cannot be accidentally sold now.|
|May 27, 2020|Added summary of items in a backpack to the description. States Empty! by default.|
|Jun 2, 2020|0.7.9|Removed summary of items in a backpack from the description.|
|Jun 2, 2020|0.7.9|You can now open backpacks when viewing your [[Ender Chest]], Inventory ([[Player]]) or other Backpacks by right-clicking.|
|Sep 8, 2020|0.9|Added Jumbo Backpack.|
|Mar 22, 2021|Fixed bug where items would be deleted if the mouse cursor was moved too fast after inserting an item into a backpack within the inventory or ender chest.|
|Apr 5, 2021|Backpacks have been temporarily disabled.|
|Apr 8, 2021|Backpacks have been re-enabled.|
|Apr 8, 2021|Backpacks have been temporarily disabled, again.|
|Apr 20, 2021|Backpacks have been re-enabled, again, and are now inaccessible outside [[Storage]].|
|Apr 20, 2021|Jumbo Backpacks now have only 45 slots.|
|Apr 20, 2021|Backpacks cannot be placed in Ender Chests anymore.|
|Jun 26, 2023|Added a /backpack [slot] command.|
|Jul 8, 2025|0.23.1|Recipes adjusted, specifically quantity of [[Enchanted Leather]].|

Item ID: ROUGH_PERIDOT_GEM; FLAWED_PERIDOT_GEM; FINE_PERIDOT_GEM; FLAWLESS_PERIDOT_GEM; PERFECT_PERIDOT_GEM
Tooltip: Collection Item; Harvested from the plentiful gemstone veins in the Crystal Hollows.; Some say that when harnessed properly, it can give its owner extra &#xE010; Health.; COMMON
Gemstones are a category of [[Mining]] [[Collections|Collection]] items. They can be applied to items with [[Gemstone Slot|Gemstone Slots]] to grant stat boosts, and are used in a variety of crafting and [[The Forge|Forging]] recipes.
## Collection
Collecting all types of Gemstones will contribute to the Gemstone [[Collections|Collection]], rather than having each individual Gemstone having its own unique collection.
Tip: click on certain slots to change the UI or go to another article!
|LVL|Required|Reward|XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|I|100|Small Gemstone Sack ([[Gemstone Sack]]) Recipe|+4|
|II|250|[[Gemstone]] Flawed Gemstone Recipe|+4|
|III|1,000|Medium Gemstone Sack ([[Gemstone Sack]]) Recipe|+4|
|IV|2,500|[[Talisman of Power]] Recipe|+4|
|V|5,000|[[Gemstone]] Fine Gemstone Recipe|+4|
|VI|25,000|[[Ring of Power]] Recipe; Large Gemstone Sack ([[Gemstone Sack]]) Recipe|+4|
|VII|100,000|Power Scroll ([[Power Scrolls]]) Recipe|+4|
|VIII|250,000|[[Enchanted Book]] (Prismatic I ([[Prismatic]])) Recipe|+4|
|IX|500,000|[[Gemstone]] Flawless Gemstone Recipe|+4|
|X|1,000,000|[[Artifact of Power]] Recipe; [[Gemstone]] Perfect Gemstone Dwarven Forge Recipe|+4|
|XI|2,000,000|[[Gemstone Gauntlet]] Recipe|+4|
## Mechanics
Gemstones come in 12 varieties: [[Gemstone|Ruby]], [[Gemstone|Amber]], [[Gemstone|Sapphire]], [[Gemstone|Jade]], [[Gemstone|Amethyst]], [[Gemstone|Topaz]], [[Gemstone|Jasper]], [[Gemstone|Opal]], [[Onyx (disambiguation)|Onyx]], [[Gemstone|Aquamarine]], [[Gemstone|Citrine]] and [[Gemstone|Peridot]].
Each Gemstone has 5 tiers, listed from lowest to highest: Rough, Flawed, Fine, Flawless and Perfect. Each tier is crafted from 80 of the previous, with the exception of Perfect, which is forged with 5 Flawless Gemstones and their respective [[Gemstone Crystals|Gemstone Crystal]].

## Obtaining
### Mining
Jade Gemstones in the [[Mithril Deposits]]
Gemstones can be mined all around the [[Crystal Hollows]], [[Smoldering Tomb]], [[Glacite Tunnels]] and [[Glacite Mineshafts]].
Mining Gemstones will drop 3-5 of the Rough tier by default. [[Pristine]] grants a chance to replace the Rough Gemstone drops with Flawed Gemstones.

### Treasure Chests
[[Gemstone|Ruby]], [[Gemstone|Amber]], [[Gemstone|Sapphire]], [[Gemstone|Jade]], [[Gemstone|Amethyst]] and [[Gemstone|Topaz]] Gemstones of Rough, Flawed and Fine qualities can be found in [[Treasure Chest|Treasure Chests]]. The possible Gemstone types depend on the location of the [[Treasure Chest]].
### Metal Detector
[[Gemstone|Ruby]], [[Gemstone|Amber]], [[Gemstone|Sapphire]], [[Gemstone|Jade]], [[Gemstone|Amethyst]] and [[Gemstone|Topaz]] Gemstones of Rough, Flawed and Fine qualities can be found using a [[Metal Detector]] in the [[Mines of Divan]].
### Mobs
Certain mobs drop Gemstones when killed.

|[[Bal]]|[Lv100]|Flawed Topaz Gemstone ([[Gemstone]])|5%|
### Crystal Nucleus
The [[Crystal Nucleus]] drops 1 Fine Gemstone of each type when completed. It can also drop additional Flawed, Fine or Flawless Gemstones of any kind.
### Frozen Corpses
[[Frozen Corpses]] can be found in [[Glacite Mineshafts]] and can be looted for a variety of rewards, including Gemstones.
### Fossil Excavator
Gemstones can be found while excavating [[Suspicious Scrap|Suspicious Scraps]] in the [[Fossil Excavator]].
### Other
[[Odawa]] sells 1 [[Gemstone|Rough Amethyst Gemstone]] for 1 [[Sludge Juice]].
Throwing the [[Eternal Flame Ring]] into the correct lava pool yields 64 (1 stack) [[Gemstone|Rough Topaz Gemstone]].
## Usage
### Gemstone Slots
Certain items have [[Gemstone Slot|Gemstone Slots]], where a corresponding gemstone can be applied. Some are already unlocked, and others require [[Coins]] and/or items to be unlocked. The Gemstone applied gives varying stat bonuses depending on its tier.
### Crafting
Flawed, Fine, and Flawless Gemstones can be crafted with 80 of the previous tiers.

## Trivia
- Gemstones are the only [[Collections|Collection]] items which have 5 tiers (Rough, Flawed, Fine, Flawless and Perfect).
- The only way to instantly mine Gemstones is with any tool that can break them and [[Armadillo Pet]]'s Tunneller ability.
- All Gemstones in the [[Crystal Hollows]] take 5 minutes to regenerate.

## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Jun 30, 2021|AHN 0.12 Round 1|Added Gemstones.|
|Jul 7, 2021|Perfect Gemstones can no longer be crafted with 80 Flawless Gemstones, and are created in [[The Forge]] instead, requiring Heart of the Mountain V (5) ([[Heart of the Mountain]]) and Gemstone X (10).|
|Mar 8, 2022|Added Opal Gemstone.|
|Mar 21, 2024|Added Onyx, Aquamarine, Citrine, and Peridot Gemstones.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Jul 14, 2021|0.12|Added Gemstones.|
|Jul 14, 2021|0.12|Added Gemstone slots to [[Aspect of the End]], [[Aspect of the Void]], [[Zombie Sword]], [[Ornate Zombie Sword]], [[Reaper Falchion]], [[Pooch Sword]], Axe of the Shredded ([[Halberd of the Shredded]]), [[Yeti Sword]], [[Midas' Sword]], [[Daedalus Blade]], [[Aspect of the Dragons]], [[Bonzo's Staff]], [[Spirit Sceptre]], [[Adaptive Blade]], [[Ice Spray Wand]], [[Livid Dagger]], [[Shadow Fury]], [[Flower of Truth]], [[Giant's Sword]], [[Titanium Drill DR-X455]], [[Titanium Drill DR-X555]], [[Topaz Drill KGR-12]], [[Blaze Armor]], [[Mastiff Armor]], [[Perfect Armor]], [[Shark Scale Armor]], [[Superior Dragon Armor]], [[Adaptive Armor]], [[Shadow Assassin Armor]], [[Necromancer Lord Armor]], and [[Sorrow Armor]].|
|Jul 14, 2021|0.12|Reduced Rough Topaz Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]) drops from Yogs ([[Yog]]) from 14-26 to 8-15.|
|Jul 14, 2021|0.12|Reduced Rough Amethyst Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]) drops from Thysts ([[Thyst]]) from 6-10 to 4-7.|
|Jul 14, 2021|0.12|Reduced Rough Jade Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]) drops from Grunts ([[Grunt]]) and their variants from 14-26 to 8-15.|
|Jul 14, 2021|0.12|Reduced Rough Sapphire Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]) drops from [Lv100] Automatons ([[Automaton]]) from 14-26 to 8-15.|
|Jul 14, 2021|0.12|Reduced Rough Sapphire Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]) drops from [Lv150] Automatons ([[Automaton]]) from 18-33 to 10-18.|
|Jul 15, 2021|Reduced [[Pristine]] from all Topaz Gemstones by 60%.|
|Jul 15, 2021|Added Gemstone slots to [[Shadow Assassin Armor]], [[Adaptive Armor]], [[Adaptive Blade]] and [[Bonzo's Staff]].|
|Aug 17, 2021|Fixed Gemstone slot buffs not stacking when 2 or more of the same Gemstone are applied to an item.|
|Sep 2, 2021|Rough -7 [[Coins]] (from 8 to 1); Flawed: -560 [[Coins]] (from 640 to 80)[Confirm]; Fine: -22,400 [[Coins]] (from 25,600 to 3,200)[Confirm]; Flawless: -448,000 [[Coins]] (from 512,000 to 64,000)[Confirm]; The changes were "accidental" and were reverted after about 1 hour.|
|Sep 13, 2021|-4 [[Coins]] base NPC sell price on all Gemstones (from 8 [[Coins]] to 4 [[Coins]]).; Rough: 8 → 4; Flawed: 640 → 320; Flawless: 512,000 → 2,048,000; Perfect: 1,000,000 → 10,240,000|
|Sep 13, 2021|Added a coin fee to removing Gemstones from items.|
|Sep 13, 2021|Added 4 new types of Gemstone slots: Combat ( Jasper ([[Gemstone]]), Sapphire ([[Gemstone]]), Ruby ([[Gemstone]]), or Amethyst ([[Gemstone]]) only), Defensive ( Ruby ([[Gemstone]]) or Amethyst ([[Gemstone]]) only), Offensive ( Jasper ([[Gemstone]]) or Sapphire ([[Gemstone]]) only), and Mining ( Jade ([[Gemstone]]), Amber ([[Gemstone]]) or Topaz ([[Gemstone]]) only).|
|Sep 13, 2021|Added Gemstone slots to many items.|
|Sep 13, 2021|All existing Gemstones on items which no longer fit into slots can be removed for free.|
|Sep 13, 2021|Item lore is now edited to display gemstone slot details: the type of slot, if it is unlocked or not, and the quality of the Gemstone inside the slot if there is.|
|Sep 13, 2021|The [[Crystal Nucleus]] is now guaranteed to drop 1 Fine Gemstone of each type on top of its rewards.|
|Sep 13, 2021|Crystal Nucleus has increased chance to drop Flawless Gemstones.|
|Sep 13, 2021|Increased the amount of stats Sapphire, Ruby and Amethyst grants. Click here ([[Gemstone/stats/Pre Sep 13 2021]]) to view the old stats.|
|Sep 13, 2021|All Flawed Gemstones rarity increased from COMMON ([[Rarity]]) to UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]]).|
|Sep 13, 2021|All Fine Gemstones rarity increased from UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]]) to RARE ([[Rarity]]).|
|Sep 13, 2021|All Flawless Gemstones rarity increased from RARE ([[Rarity]]) to EPIC ([[Rarity]]).|
|Sep 13, 2021|All Perfect Gemstones rarity increased from EPIC ([[Rarity]]) to LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]]).|
|Nov 27, 2021|Disabled the ability to sell Flawless and Perfect Gemstones to NPCs.|
|Apr 20, 2022|0.13|Added Opal Gemstone.|
|Sep 10, 2022|Flawless and Perfect Gemstones are no longer stackable.|
|Apr 9, 2024|0.20|Added Onyx, Aquamarine, Citrine, and Citrine Gemstones.|
|Apr 9, 2024|0.20|Perfect Opal Gemstone now requires the Opal Crystal to forge.|
|May 28, 2024|0.20.2|Special ores, such as [[Mithril]] and [[Gemstone]] Gemstones, now respawn even if the chunk they're in gets unloaded.|
|Mar 18, 2025|0.22|Changed Aquamarine Gemstones to grant [[Fishing Speed]] instead of [[Sea Creature Chance]].|
|Aug 5, 2025|0.23.3|Doubled the stats from Citrine Gemstones.|

Distillates are [[Rarity|RARE]] items used to craft [[Inferno Minion Fuel]].
## List
There are five different Distillates, as listed below.
|Slot|Item|
|-|-|
|Magma Cream Distillate|Magma Cream Distillate|
|Blaze Rod Distillate|Blaze Rod Distillate|
|Nether Wart Distillate|Nether Wart Distillate|
|Glowstone Distillate|Glowstone Distillate|
|Gabagool Distillate|Gabagool Distillate|
## Obtaining
### Mob Drops
Distillates are obtained as a [[Drop Chance|5Rare]] drop from [[Inferno Demonlord]] Tier II+ with at least [[Blaze Slayer|Blaze Slayer III (3)]]. The amount dropped varies depending on the tier of the boss killed.
[[Distillates|Gabagool Distillate]] has the following drop chances:
|Slayer Tier|Amount|Drop Chance|
|-|-|-|
|Tier II|2|5.19%|
|Tier III|6-10|4.87%|
|Tier IV|16-24|4.43%|
[[Distillates|Magma Cream Distillate]], [[Distillates|Blaze Rod Distillate]], [[Distillates|Nether Wart Distillate]], and [[Distillates|Glowstone Distillate]] can only be dropped from [[Loot Sharing]] with [More Info Needed] (More infomation needed; When this info is added, remove this.) chance.
## Usage
### Crafting
Distillates are used to craft [[Inferno Minion Fuel]].
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|RARE ([[Rarity]]) [[Inferno Minion Fuel]]; ( [[Magma Cream]])|None|1x [[Fuel Gabagool]] 2x [[Inferno Fuel Block]] 6x Magma Cream Distillate ([[Distillates]])|Recipe: [[Distillates]] ×6 + [[Inferno Fuel Block]] ×2 + [[Fuel Gabagool]] → [[Inferno Minion Fuel]]|
|EPIC ([[Rarity]]) [[Inferno Minion Fuel]]; ( [[Magma Cream]])|None|1x [[Heavy Gabagool]] 2x [[Inferno Fuel Block]] 6x Magma Cream Distillate ([[Distillates]])|Recipe: [[Distillates]] ×6 + [[Inferno Fuel Block]] ×2 + [[Heavy Gabagool]] → [[Inferno Minion Fuel]]|
|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]]) [[Inferno Minion Fuel]]; ( [[Magma Cream]])|None|1x [[Hypergolic Gabagool]] 2x [[Inferno Fuel Block]] 6x Magma Cream Distillate ([[Distillates]])|Recipe: [[Distillates]] ×6 + [[Inferno Fuel Block]] ×2 + [[Hypergolic Gabagool]] → [[Inferno Minion Fuel]]|

# Precursor Relic
Categories: Crafting ingredients, Catacombs, Dungeons, Dungeon Items, Mob Drops
[Precursor Relic]
Precursor Relics are items obtained from killing [[The Watcher|Undead Giants]] in the [[The Watcher|Blood Room]] while in [[The Catacombs - Floor VII]]. They are used to craft the different variants of the [[Necron's Blade (Unrefined)|Necron's Blade]] and [[Wither Armor]].
## Obtaining
Precursor Relics are dropped by the [[The Watcher|Undead Giants]]. There are four Precursor Relics:
|Slot|Item|Drops from|
|-|-|-|
|[[Bigfoot's Bola]]|[[Bigfoot's Bola]]|Bigfoot|
|[[Jolly Pink Rock]]|[[Jolly Pink Rock]]|Jolly Pink Giant|
|[[L.A.S.R.'s Eye]]|[[L.A.S.R.'s Eye]]|L.A.S.R.|
|[[Diamante's Handle]]|[[Diamante's Handle]]|The Diamond Giant|
Each Undead Giant has a 50% chance to drop its associated Precursor Relic.
## Usage
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|Maxor's Helmet ([[Maxor's Armor]])|None|8 [[Bigfoot's Bola]] Wither Helmet ([[Wither Armor]]) variant|Recipe: [[Bigfoot's Bola]] ×8 + [[Wither Armor]]/[[Storm's Armor]]/[[Goldor's Armor]]/[[Necron's Armor]] → [[Maxor's Armor]]|
|Maxor's Chestplate ([[Maxor's Armor]])|None|8 [[Bigfoot's Bola]] Wither Chestplate ([[Wither Armor]]) variant|Recipe: [[Bigfoot's Bola]] ×8 + [[Wither Armor]]/[[Storm's Armor]]/[[Goldor's Armor]]/[[Necron's Armor]] → [[Maxor's Armor]]|
